Choice: TERMS-021: a business data page in English and Spanish covering what is kept about businesses and team phones and for how long, who can see it, that call logs hold no recordings, that Ask Hava never asks about workers, that data goes to police or others only when a law requires it (such as a subpoena or warrant), and that Casey does not use Ask Hava data in any other job. Every line must be exactly true; an attorney review is optional but recommended. Reason: what is collected and shared must be stated (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:214`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:216`).
- **D-204** Question (plan B17, cards D6 and D16): no named contact for charges or reviews, and no spending limit. Casey: yes. Choice: TERMS-022: the business terms and every receipt name Casey as the contact for charge questions with a 1-business-day reply (a setting); BIZ-027: a sign-up in needs review gets his decision within 1 business day (a setting), a service target that blocks nothing; FEE-041: an optional business-set monthly fee limit, above which a Confirm is not charged and the reply gives the portal link. Reason: lowers dispute risk; receipts already go out for every charge (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:252`).
- **D-205** Question (plan B18, card D17): keep "the fee stands once you've talked"? Casey: yes. Choice: no "didn't book" credit; FEE-023 and FEE-024 unchanged; the plain-words box (BIZ-030) says "It's an introduction fee for a homeowner who chose you." Reason: a credit is easy to game off-platform and recreates refund fights (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:98`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:238`).
- **D-206** Question (plan B19, sim_v3 S1): during a texting suspension a homeowner with no email gets no safety text. Casey: yes (his call, lean yes, with a lawyer's check). Choice: TXT-024: one automated voice call from the Ask Hava number reads the generic safety text, only to a homeowner with no email whose own message sent during the suspension matched an emergency keyword; CALL-019 sets the script (the CALL-014 opening, the safety wording, no job details). Never for quote updates; logged; the critical alert to Casey stays. It is not a second number, so TXT-022 holds. The lawyer's check is an open item for Casey. Reason: the call answers the homeowner's own just-sent request (prior express consent) rather than leaning on the narrow "emergency purposes" exemption (47 CFR 64.1200(f)(4), as cited in the plan); the safety text stays generic (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:196`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:75`).
- **D-207** Question (plan B20, sim_v3 S2): a backup person for safe mode? Casey: yes to launching without one. Choice: OPS-002 unchanged; OPS-026: an owner "away until <date>" setting shows businesses when reviews and disputes will be answered and changes no timer, so the 1-business-day promises stay honest. Revisit a backup person after 30 active businesses. Reason: no backup person for now (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:73`).

**Spec changes from the business-side fix plan:** `job-cards.md` CARD-007 changed, CARD-019 added; `licensing.md` LIC-001, LIC-008, LIC-011, LIC-036 changed, LIC-037 and LIC-038 added; `businesses.md` BIZ-017, BIZ-018, BIZ-020, BIZ-021 changed, BIZ-022 to BIZ-031 added; `quotes-pick.md` PICK-019, PICK-024, PICK-026, PICK-033, PICK-034 changed, PICK-037 to PICK-039 added; `fees.md` FEE-010, FEE-015 changed, FEE-039 to FEE-041 added; `recurring.md` REC-009 changed, REC-011 added; `messaging.md` TXT-024 changed, TXT-026 added; `calls.md` CALL-012 changed, CALL-019 added; `consent.md` CON-019 changed; `security.md` SEC-004 changed; `ops-recovery.md` OPS-026 and OPS-027 added; `terms.md` TERMS-021 and TERMS-022 added; `website.md` WEB-005 changed, WEB-018 to WEB-021 added; `wording.md` WORD-016 and WORD-017 added. Catalog: worked example W20 changed to $75, W33 added, and the $75 recurring cap applied in `scripts/check_catalog.py`. Tests: see `docs/test-design.md` (TD rows naming D-172 to D-207).

## Three-angle fix plan, October 28, 2026

The sim_v6 business, homeowner and owner findings (`/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v6/`) and the fix plan built on them (`/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v7/Three_Angle_Fix_Plan.md`, research in `01_research.md` there), all outside this repository, used AI-simulated businesses, homeowners and an owner model, not real people; numbers marked [sim] there are simulated, and legal-fit notes are research-based judgments, not legal advice. Casey, Oct 28, 2026: "Ok do whatever research you need to to make the most ideal changes if any and then run it again." Priorities applied in order: safety and legal fit; Casey's time (about 20 minutes a week, nothing that can't wait 12 hours except real safety items); homeowner completion and trust; business sign-up and retention; profit. Nothing here spends money, adds a vendor or data processor, or signs an agreement; those items are listed in `docs/for-casey-review.md`. The fee stays a fixed introduction fee by job size, with no ranking, paid placement, pricing, recommending or vetting; pest control is never advertised; ROC and city lists stay verification-only; no marketing texts; Gate P and build_hold are unchanged.

- **D-208** Question (plan S1, S2; homeowner #2): an AC failure in the heat with no danger word waits like any job, and nothing asks how soon the job is needed. Choice: INT-030: every request asks "How soon do you need this? 1) Today, it's urgent 2) In the next few days 3) No rush", the answer is a job-card field (CARD-007), and "Today" leads to the urgent-or-regular choice (INT-006); INT-031: a message naming an AC, cooler or swamp cooler with heat words (hot, no AC, not cooling, or a temperature of 85 or more), or a "Today" answer on an AC or cooler job, gets the heat-safety text at once at any hour (QH-004) and is offered the emergency path; the heat text is generic ("If anyone is confused, very weak, or not sweating, call 911 now. Go somewhere with AC if you can; fans don't cool enough in extreme heat. Drink water. Ask Hava is not an emergency service."), does not hold the request and never names 211. Reason: most indoor heat deaths in Maricopa County in 2023 had a broken AC; the NWS says heat stroke needs 911; 211 Arizona lost live operators in August 2026 (plan research R14 to R18); emergencies offer urgent or regular (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:86`).
- **D-209** Question (plan S3; homeowner #18, #19): the gas path doesn't send people to the gas company first, and the report proposed dropping the gas line from non-gas safety texts. Choice: INT-004: a gas or carbon-monoxide match adds "Call your gas company now, before getting quotes. Keep the appliance off until a licensed pro checks it."; the generic gas line stays in every safety text. Dropping it is rejected. Reason: the safety text stays generic with the gas line (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:196`).
- **D-210** Question (plan S4; owner L9): during a texting suspension a homeowner with an email on file gets the safety text only by email, which may sit unread. Choice: TXT-024: the one safety voice call also goes when an email is on file, still only for the homeowner's own emergency message sent during the suspension; opted-out numbers are not called (a lawyer item; the critical alert still fires, CON-017). Reason: same footing as D-206, the homeowner's own just-sent request (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:196`).
- **D-211** Question (plan S5; owner L9, G1): a critical safety alert wakes Casey even when the safety call already reached someone. Choice: OPS-028: the critical alert is acknowledged automatically, logged and listed in the digest when the safety voice call was answered by a person; otherwise it pushes as before and Casey makes one call reading the same generic script. Reason: safe mode and alerts wait for Casey with no backup person (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:73`).
- **D-212** Question (plan S6; homeowner #8): only rentals are asked who will meet the business. Choice: PICK-025: every pick asks "Who will let the business in? 1) Me 2) Someone else 3) A lockbox", passes an on-site first name and number to the picked business only with the release, never texts that person (CON-021), and never stores a lock or gate code ("tell the business the code on the call"). Reason: extends the rental question (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:614`); release only on a paid pick (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:151`).
- **D-213** Question (plan S7; homeowner #3b, safety 3): the first business call comes from an unknown number. Choice: TXT-027: the who's-calling text names the business, the masked number and the call window, says the number works for call-backs for 30 days, and adds "Ask for the price and any changes in writing before work starts. Don't pay in full up front." Reason: 80% don't answer unknown numbers but 67% check voicemail (Pew 2020); FTC contractor advice (plan R9, v6 B10); important texts include who's calling (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:187`).
- **D-214** Question (plan S8; homeowner #13): privacy words are true but easy to read as broader than they are, and homeowners have no data page. Choice: WORD-018 adds "Ask Hava never gives your number to any business. If a business asks for it on the call, that's your choice." beside the WORD-007 lines; PRIV-011: the delete confirmation says the number stays locked for 5 years only as proof of texting permission and opt-out; TERMS-008: while AI-008 holds, the policy says "No AI reads your messages today."; TERMS-023: a homeowner data page in English and Spanish. Reason: say exactly what is shared (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:191`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:214`).
- **D-215** Question (plan S9; homeowner #14): nothing limits what a picked business does with the homeowner's details. Choice: TERMS-024: the business terms say "Use the homeowner's details only for this job and follow-up about it. No marketing lists, no resale.", and a homeowner can report a breach with PROBLEM. Reason: business terms (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:212`).
- **D-216** Question (plan S10; homeowner #22): a tenant may pay for a repair the landlord owes. Choice: TXT-028: after a RENT answer the homeowner gets "If you rent, your landlord may be responsible for this repair. Check your lease or ask your landlord before you choose." as information, not legal advice; the 7-day WAIT option is rejected (D-269). Reason: A.R.S. 33-1364 tenant remedies need notice to the landlord (plan v6 F1); rental flag (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:89`).
- **D-217** Question (plan S11; business 4.6): the business data page doesn't promise notice of legal demands or say retention in days. Choice: TERMS-021 adds "We'll tell you about any legal demand for your business's data unless the law forbids it." and the retention, in days, of business records, team phones and call logs. Reason: what is collected and kept must be stated (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:214`).
- **D-218** Question (plan T1; owner report 1-4, G15): today's 24-hour and 1-business-day promises need a daily check, and any alert may push. Choice: OPS-029: Casey works in one weekly 30-minute window and a 10-minute mid-week check; only four things push to his phone: a critical safety alert not acknowledged by OPS-028, the app still failing its health check 15 minutes after the first failure, a Twilio suspension, and a ledger difference still present at the next nightly check; only critical alerts may break Do Not Disturb. Reason: batching notifications lowered stress while zero notifications raised anxiety (Fitz et al. 2019); interruptions add stress (Mark et al. 2008) (plan R20, R21); alerts wait for Casey (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:73`).
- **D-219** Question (plan T2; owner L10): information-only items each alert Casey. Choice: OPS-030: a weekly digest email lists automatic activations, self-cleared alerts, no-shows, automatic pauses (no review needed), license-unknown counts, opt-out rates, complaints with replies, dispute filings, unknown charges already refunded, unreadable business texts and call-backs due, with counts and job numbers and never homeowner contact details (PRIV-006). Reason: owner report L10; the weekly summary uses counts (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:294`).
- **D-220** Question (plan T3; owner L4, G16): vendor alerts need Casey's acknowledgment even after they clear, so a trip puts charges in safe mode. Choice: OPS-023: Stripe or webhook alerts clear after 2 hours with no errors, health alerts when the check passes again, and ledger alerts when the next nightly check matches; each is acknowledged automatically, logged and listed in the digest. Reason: the text-delivery alert already clears itself (D-134); safe mode is for unacknowledged problems (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:375`).
- **D-221** Question (plan T4; owner L6, L11, G14): weekly spot checks and money reads were 40% of Casey's time. Choice: OPS-009: the spot check of 10 random cards is monthly (a setting) and there is no weekly money-view read (the nightly ledger check alerts); FEE-033 and OPS-027 flags are shown for the monthly review; LIC-023 blocks on its own and needs no review. Reason: the blueprint's weekly check becomes monthly (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:410`).
- **D-222** Question (plan T5; owner L8, homeowner #17): every complaint alerts at once, and a PROBLEM reply goes into a void. Choice: OPS-005, BIZ-012, JOB-016: complaints and PROBLEM replies are listed in the digest and decided once after the BIZ-029 reply box closes, except those with a safety word (unsafe, threat, stole, hurt, injured, police, fire, or their Spanish words), which alert at once; TXT-029: the PROBLEM reply says "A person reads every problem report within 7 days. Ask Hava can't settle disputes about work or price." with the ROC complaint link; the 3-in-90-days pause is unchanged. Reason: complaints alert Casey (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:462`); no fee changes (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:238`).
- **D-223** Question (plan T6; owner L2, L3, G4): the 1-business-day review and charge-question targets force a daily check, and "business day" is undefined. Choice: BIZ-027 and TERMS-022: 4 days (settings), counted in calendar days; the targets still block nothing. This revises D-204's number only. Reason: the weekly and mid-week windows are at most 4 days apart; the business-side fixes (D-253 to D-256) cut the flags that need review (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:115`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:212`).
- **D-224** Question (plan T7; business 4.6, homeowner #4): "Who runs it" leaves out the day job, and homeowners have no such block. Choice: WEB-021 adds "Casey has a full-time day job. Sign-up reviews and charge questions are answered within 4 days, and this page shows when he's away."; WEB-022: a homeowner "Who runs Ask Hava" block on the home and help pages with the same facts; police work is still not mentioned (C8). Reason: Ask Hava LLC and its mailing address are public (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:227`).
- **D-225** Question (plan T8; owner L3, business gap 6): charge questions take Casey's time and receipts don't say tax is included. Choice: FEE-031: every receipt says "Tax included." and links a "Why was I charged?" page for that charge (job number, quote, tier, how the fee basis was set, refund rules). Reason: receipts for every charge (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:252`); fees are tax-included (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:248`).
- **D-226** Question (plan T9; owner L1 without a purchase): call-backs carry a 24-hour deadline. Choice: INT-023 and TXT-015: a call-back for a homeowner who can't be reached by text or email is due within 24 hours for a job marked "Today, it's urgent" or emergency and within 4 days otherwise; CON-016: an opted-out homeowner who writes again without START is emailed if possible and otherwise listed in the digest, with no call-back (an emergency word still raises CON-017's critical alert). An answering service is listed for Casey. Reason: the blueprint's 24-hour promise (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:538`) is kept where waiting can hurt; Casey's call says he can't do daily checks.
- **D-227** Question (plan T10; owner G9): calls to the Ask Hava number have no defined handling. Choice: CALL-020: a recorded greeting ("This is Ask Hava LLC. We work by text. To start a request, text this number. For help, text HELP. If this is an emergency, hang up and call 911."), no voicemail, no recording and no ring-through to Casey. Reason: calls are never recorded (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:141`); the identity-call opening (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:182`).
- **D-228** Question (plan T11; owner L12, G21): business texts the app can't read all go to Casey. Choice: BIZ-032: they get an automatic reply with the portal link and HELP; one with a question mark or a word such as charge, refund, wrong or problem is also emailed to Casey and listed in the digest. Reason: a business that asks about a live pick already gets an automatic reply (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:90`).
- **D-229** Question (plan T12; owner L7): a dispute needs Casey to file evidence by a deadline. Choice: FEE-027: the evidence pack from the audit log is submitted to Stripe automatically 2 days before the due date unless Casey submitted it first, and listed in the digest. Reason: evidence comes from the audit log (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:337`).
- **D-230** Question (plan T13; owner G2, L5): no alert and no fallback for a broken license-list reader. Choice: LIC-039: a list that fails to load for 12 hours (a setting) raises an alert for Casey's next window, and while it fails the throttled single live lookup runs for each active business so verified statuses keep updating; a maintainer is listed for Casey. Reason: failed lookups are "unknown" and never guessed (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:194`); the live lookup is the fallback where no list exists (D-056).
- **D-231** Question (plan T14; owner L13, G13): every shared-number event alerts. Choice: CALL-017: at most one number-pool alert is open at a time; the pool still starts at 10 numbers (more cost money, listed for Casey). Reason: the app never buys numbers (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:61`).
- **D-232** Question (plan T15; owner G3): nobody sees the monthly test restore result. Choice: OPS-013: its result is listed in the digest and a failed restore raises a High alert. Reason: monthly test restore (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:469`).
- **D-233** Question (plan H1; homeowner #1, #12, #16): homeowners can't compare quotes or ask anything before picking. Choice: PICK-040: every quote carries required quote facts (trip fee counts toward the repair; parts and materials; permit; earliest visit; speaks Spanish on the phone, on site or no), shown in a fixed order in the homeowner's language, with plain definitions of each quote type and, in Spanish, the business's words labeled "Palabras del negocio (en inglés)" (PICK-002, PICK-003). The tap-to-ask menu is rejected (D-269). Reason: the business's own facts, never Ask Hava pricing (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:237`); original wording always shown (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:200`).
- **D-234** Question (plan H2; homeowner #5): the spec doesn't say whether the business name shows, and the prototype has a "Read their reviews" link. Choice: PICK-041: each quote card shows the business's public business name and the same fixed line on every card: "Ask Hava shows no reviews and doesn't rank businesses. To check this business yourself: look up its license, search its name, or ask it for local references."; PICK-010 clarifies that the page shows no homeowner contact details and no business phone or address. Reason: the same line on every card ranks nobody (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:189`); "Check it yourself" (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:192`).
- **D-235** Question (plan H3; homeowner #3a): the first call comes at a time the homeowner didn't choose. Choice: PICK-018: at the pick the homeowner also chooses a call window (any time 8 a.m. to 8 p.m., mornings, 12 to 1, or after 5 p.m.), shown to the business with the release and in TXT-026's reminders; FEE-022: only attempts inside that window count toward the unreachable refund. Reason: Angi lets homeowners share call times (plan R3); contact choice at the pick (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:408`).
- **D-236** Question (plan H4; homeowner #3c, business 4.1(3)): a missed first call leaves the homeowner unaware. Choice: TXT-030: after a business's first unanswered call attempt the homeowner gets one text naming the business, the request number and the masked call-back number, under quiet hours; FEE-022: the refund fires only if that text or its email copy went out. Reason: it is about the homeowner's own request (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:181`); refund rule (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:334`).
- **D-237** Question (plan H5; homeowner #4, #11): the first text is a legal paragraph, "is this a person?" gets the same paragraph, and natural agreement words are refused. Choice: CON-031: the consent text opens "Ask Hava: Got your message. We're a local quote service in Lake Havasu City. Free for you. Our texts are automated; reply HELP to reach a person."; CON-027: a reply asking who this is or whether it's a person gets the consent text once more with "Ask Hava is a local service. Our texts are automated. A person reads HELP messages." on top, in one text, and a re-send goes in Spanish when the reply reads as Spanish; CON-005 adds fine, "go ahead", "sounds good", "de acuerdo", "está bien", claro, dale and listo. Reason: consent first on every path (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:113`); truthful answer when asked (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:197`).
- **D-238** Question (plan H6; homeowner #6): the address question gives no reason. Choice: INT-027: the question says "Only the business you pick gets this, after you pick it. We never ask about immigration status or ID."; moving it to the pick is rejected (D-269). Reason: privacy words (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:191`).
- **D-239** Question (plan H7; homeowner #7): people who can't open links can't see quotes. Choice: TXT-031: "LIST" (and LISTA) gets the quotes by text in arrival order, one line each with number, business name, type and amount and earliest visit, up to 10 per text, ending with how to reply "<n> YES"; TXT-016: the first-quote text includes that quote's type and amount. Reason: "<n> YES" already picks by text (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:13`); first-quote text (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:82`).
- **D-240** Question (plan H8; homeowner #9): the check-ins have no answer for "booked, not done", "ongoing" or "hired someone else". Choice: JOB-028: LATER (asks again at day 14), ONGOING (recurring service started; no more check-ins; the job closes as Done) and OTHER (hired someone else; closes with no no-show), with Spanish MÁS TARDE, CONTINUO and OTRO; JOB-015 accepts LISTO as DONE. Reason: day-3 and day-14 check-ins (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:176`).
- **D-241** Question (plan H9; homeowner #10): homeowners don't know when or how many quotes to expect, and a no-quote close can be silent. Choice: TXT-032: at the end of intake "Sent to <N> local businesses. They have until <time, day> to quote." (with "at 8 a.m." when held overnight); quote updates say "<k> of <N> businesses have quoted so far"; JOB-010: the no-quotes text offers "1) Keep waiting 2) Add details 3) Close"; JOB-011: the close always says why. Reason: update texts (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:84`); timeouts (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:111`).
- **D-242** Question (plan H10; homeowner #15): uncovered work is refused late. Choice: INT-016: the category check is the first intake step after consent; WEB-023: a "What we cover now" page lists open categories and "not yet" ones (never pest) and says Canadian numbers work and roaming charges may apply. Reason: an immediate reply for uncovered requests (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:50`); pest never advertised (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:239`); Canada is in the area-code table (D-100).
- **D-243** Question (plan H11; homeowner #20, D-049): an email-contact homeowner can be closed out by unanswered calls. Choice: FEE-022 and JOB-027: when the homeowner chose email, at 72 hours they are asked "Has <Business> emailed you? Reply YES or NO."; YES stops the refund and the close, while NO or no reply leaves the rule as before. Reason: the refund rule exists for unreachable homeowners (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:334`).
- **D-244** Question (plan H12; homeowner #16): no written copy of the accepted quote. Choice: PICK-042: after a Confirm the homeowner gets the accepted quote (type, amount, quote facts and the business's words) by email if on file, and the quote page keeps it until its token expires. Reason: original wording always shown (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:200`); FTC: get it in writing (plan v6 B10).
- **D-245** Question (plan H13; homeowner #4, #5): the prototype says "concierge", "Read their reviews" and "a new Ask Hava feature", and arrival numbers look like ranks. Choice: WORD-019 bans those three phrases; WEB-018 covers homeowner pages too (words from the spec, not the prototype); WORD-005 adds "#1 means first received, not first choice." Reason: no claims of judgment (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:194`); order received (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:189`).
- **D-246** Question (plan H14; homeowner smaller items): own-or-rent misses lot renters and second homes, and two jobs in one text are merged. Choice: INT-028 adds "Own the home, rent the lot" and "Second or winter home"; INT-032: a first message naming two trades starts the first and replies "That's 2 jobs. We'll start with <first>. Reply 2 to start <second> too." Reason: one job per trade (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:98`).
- **D-247** Question (plan H15; homeowner smaller items): the pick code looks like a scam pattern. Choice: PICK-013: the code text reads "Your code to choose a quote is <code>. Only type it on askhava.com. We never ask for it by phone." where Twilio Verify allows custom wording; PICK-015: the quote page shows "or reply <n> YES" by every Choose button. Reason: code and text picks (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:312`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:13`).
- **D-248** Question (plan B1; business 4.1, 4.7, 4.8, 4.11): "the fee stands" is the top business friction and the plain-words box is long. Choice: BIZ-030 adds a 3-line summary on top and "The homeowner sees every quote and picks one business: you. It isn't shared with anyone else.", "Repeat picks from the same homeowner are free for 180 days after the job closes.", what happens after the 12-month lock, and "Statements download as CSV." No credit is added (D-205 stands). Reason: industry practice gives no "didn't hire" credit (plan R1, R2); fee wording (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:238`).
- **D-249** Question (plan B2; business 4.11, gap 8): no city-license guide, no safety tip for solo workers, no opening rule for coming-later trades. Choice: WEB-020 adds the city-license guide (cost, time, link) to the handyman and no-license sample cards, a solo-worker safety tip on the business help page, and "Opens when there are enough requests; <n> businesses waiting" for coming-later trades. Reason: city list for no-license trades (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:37`).
- **D-250** Question (plan B3; business 4.1(2)): businesses can't see how often picks become jobs. Choice: BIZ-025 adds "Picked → finished" from the homeowners' DONE, NO and OTHER replies. Reason: the portal shows the business's own numbers (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:393`).
- **D-251** Question (plan B4; business 4.1(4)): no data trigger for revisiting "the fee stands". Choice: OPS-031: the monthly review flags a trade, with no automatic action, when over 30% of its connected jobs end NO or OTHER at day 3 in each of 2 consecutive months (settings); a limited "hired no one" credit then comes back to Casey as a decision. Reason: D-205 stands; a credit is a fee change (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:98`).
- **D-252** Question (plan B5; business 4.2, D-192): 2 business hours to Confirm is too short for solo owners. Choice: PICK-043: "HOLD <job>" from the owner's or a "Confirm and billing" phone adds 2 business hours (a setting) once per pick on a non-emergency job, and the homeowner gets "The business you picked is confirming. You'll hear by <time>. You can still choose another quote on your page."; PICK-019, JOB-013 and BIZ-028 follow. Reason: Airbnb gives hosts 24 hours on requests (plan R4); the window is a setting (D-192; `sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:174`).
- **D-253** Question (plan B6; business 4.3(2)): review status goes only to the dead record contact. Choice: BIZ-020: status messages (never the code) also go to the sign-up email and, by text, to the phone the business entered with its CON-019 consent. Reason: the code still goes only to public-record contacts (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:93`).
- **D-254** Question (plan B7; business 4.3(3)): a letter goes to an old address without warning. Choice: BIZ-026: before a letter, sign-up shows the town and ZIP it would go to and offers "Stop, I'll fix my record first" with the ROC update guide and the usual delay; when a daily list shows a new contact, that contact is texted or emailed "We see your updated record. Tap to finish sign-up." Reason: the identity chain is unchanged (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:93`).
- **D-255** Question (plan B8; business 4.3(4), owner section 7): city-list names are often a trade name or the owner's name, which creates flags. Choice: LIC-011: a match on the listed business name, trade name (DBA) or owner name counts, and a business may enter its city license number when the list has one (checked at build, as D-190 does). Reason: the city list verifies the business's own name (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:37`).
- **D-256** Question (plan B9; business 4.3(5)): BIZ-016 flags "switched-off categories only" while BIZ-021 sends those businesses to the waitlist. Choice: BIZ-016 drops that flag. Reason: one rule (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:35`).
- **D-257** Question (plan B10; business 4.4): cards lack the facts each trade needs, and big shops can't skip small work. Choice: LIC-040: listed trades get multiple-choice card-field intake questions (pool size and condition; roof type, stories and leak location; moves: bedrooms, stairs or elevator, distance band; boats: length band and in water, on a lift or on a trailer; appliances: type, brand list and "error code showing"; windows: count band and stories; home watch: visit schedule), never free-text model numbers; LIC-041: plumbing, HVAC, electrical, roofing and painting split job types into repair, replace and new install where the license allows, so a business can untick some (BIZ-004). Reason: catalog fields (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:35`); structured answers are safer than free text (D-202).
- **D-258** Question (plan B11; business 4.5, X02): the owner isn't told when staff confirm or link a phone. Choice: BIZ-033: the owner gets a text and an email when a non-owner phone confirms a pick or links a phone; an "owner approves fees over $__" setting is rejected (D-269). Reason: staff phones are linked by the owner (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:374`); account notices under business consent (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:181`).
- **D-259** Question (plan B12; business 4.5, 4.11): a technician can PAUSE the whole account, and Spanish keyword forms don't work. Choice: BIZ-028: PAUSE, BUSY, RESUME and HOLD work only from the owner's or a "Confirm and billing" phone; Spanish PAUSA, OCUPADO and REANUDAR work too; PICK-033 accepts SÍ and CONFIRMAR. Reason: Spanish templates for every text kind (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:88`).
- **D-260** Question (plan B13; business 4.5): a removed member's linked phones keep working. Choice: SEC-004 and CALL-012: removing a member ends their sessions and unlinks every phone they linked. Reason: staff phones are the owner's to control (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:374`).
- **D-261** Question (plan B14; business 4.7, gaps 2 and 8): waitlisted businesses have no path to active and their lock can run out first. Choice: BIZ-021: when a trade opens each waitlisted business gets one account notice by email, and by text only with consent ("<Trade> is open on Ask Hava. Finish sign-up here; your join date is kept."); BIZ-031: a waitlist version of the done screen; FEE-040: 12 months from the later of the join date and the trade's opening date. Reason: join date kept (D-175); account notices, not marketing (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:181`).
- **D-262** Question (plan B15; business 4.9): the fee shows before submitting only for trip-fee quotes. Choice: PICK-039: the quote form shows the fee for every quote before submitting, with the basis (range midpoint, recurring months and cap). Reason: fee tiers (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:59`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:61`).
- **D-263** Question (plan B16; business 4.10, X01): in small trades the loss text's count plus "Sent to N" lets a business guess who quoted. Choice: PICK-026: when the card went to 4 or fewer businesses, the loss text says "Several businesses quoted" instead of the number. Reason: coarse, nameless bid feedback (OECD guidance, plan v5).
- **D-264** Question (plan B17; business 4.11, gap 4; owner G8): the monthly fee limit is hidden and a blocked Confirm leaves the pick hanging. Choice: FEE-041: the limit field shows on the card-authorization screen; a Confirm stopped by the limit ends that pick at once, the homeowner is told the business couldn't take it and can choose another quote. Reason: card authorization (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:252`).
- **D-265** Question (plan B18; business 4.11, X03): whether the $75 cap follows any typed period. Choice: FEE-039: the cap applies to the category's recurring job types only, never to a period typed on a one-time job type. Reason: recurring rule (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:60`).
- **D-266** Question (plan B19; business 4.11, P17): call reminders confuse businesses whose homeowner chose email. Choice: TXT-026: for email-contact jobs the reminder says "The homeowner chose email; your 2 call attempts still count for the refund." and names the call window (D-235). Reason: the masked line still opens for email jobs (D-049).
- **D-267** Question (plan B20; business gap 5): read-back and "since" lines are written only for the ROC. Choice: LIC-027 and LIC-037 cover licenses from other state agencies too ("<Agency> license since <year>" when the record has a date). Reason: other-agency license lines (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:203`).
- **D-268** Question (plan 2.5; business 1.3): the pitch and flyers still carry errors. Choice: the pitch lives outside this repository, so the exact lines to fix by hand are listed in `docs/for-casey-review.md`; no spec rule changes beyond WEB-018. Reason: the spec stops the build from copying them (D-172).
- **D-269** Question (plan section 3): which proposals are rejected or deferred. Choice: not adopted now: the tap-to-ask menu (quote facts instead), up to 3 site-visit picks, tenant WAIT, a homeowner card preview, "My businesses", the address at the pick, dropping the gas line from non-gas safety texts, storing lock codes, owner fee approval, a 13-month repeat fingerprint, a "hired no one" credit now, an email-and-voice mode for landline businesses, an hourly-plus-minimum quote type, expiring BUSY or the free win, safety calls to opted-out numbers, automated status voice calls; and, because they cost money, add a processor or need a signature, an answering service, a maintainer, 5 more masked numbers, carrier registration and branded caller ID, and the zero-retention agreement (all listed for Casey). Reason: each conflicts with a higher priority, a hard rule or the no-spend instruction (plan section 3).

**Spec changes from the three-angle fix plan:** `intake.md` INT-004, INT-006, INT-016, INT-023, INT-027, INT-028 changed, INT-030 to INT-032 added; `consent.md` CON-005, CON-016, CON-027 changed, CON-031 added; `quiet-hours.md` QH-004 changed; `messaging.md` TXT-014, TXT-015, TXT-016, TXT-024, TXT-026 changed, TXT-027 to TXT-032 added; `job-cards.md` CARD-007 changed; `quotes-pick.md` PICK-002, PICK-003, PICK-010, PICK-013, PICK-015, PICK-018, PICK-019, PICK-025, PICK-026, PICK-033, PICK-039 changed, PICK-040 to PICK-043 added; `lifecycle.md` JOB-010, JOB-011, JOB-013, JOB-015, JOB-016, JOB-027 changed, JOB-028 added; `fees.md` FEE-022, FEE-027, FEE-031, FEE-033, FEE-039, FEE-040, FEE-041 changed; `calls.md` CALL-012, CALL-017 changed, CALL-020 added; `privacy.md` PRIV-011 changed; `licensing.md` LIC-011, LIC-027, LIC-037 changed, LIC-039 to LIC-041 added; `businesses.md` BIZ-012, BIZ-016, BIZ-020, BIZ-021, BIZ-025 to BIZ-028, BIZ-030, BIZ-031 changed, BIZ-032 and BIZ-033 added; `wording.md` WORD-005 changed, WORD-018 and WORD-019 added; `security.md` SEC-004 changed; `ops-recovery.md` OPS-005, OPS-009, OPS-013, OPS-023, OPS-027 changed, OPS-028 to OPS-031 added; `website.md` WEB-018, WEB-020, WEB-021 changed, WEB-022 and WEB-023 added; `terms.md` TERMS-008, TERMS-021, TERMS-022 changed, TERMS-023 and TERMS-024 added. Tests: see `docs/test-design.md` (TD rows naming D-208 to D-267).

## Fix round 8, October 28, 2026

Casey, Oct 28, 2026: "Ok fix everything we need to." Inputs, all outside this repository: the sim_v7 homeowner, business and owner findings (`/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v7/`, AI-simulated people, not real ones; [sim] numbers are simulated), ChatGPT's pre-launch research and Grok's name and competition research (both Oct 27, 2026), and new safety research; the plan is `/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v8/Fix_Round_8_Plan.md`. Priorities in order: safety, Casey's time, businesses, homeowners, legal fit from the outside research. Which messages go by text and which by other channels, and the Twilio campaign wording, are left for a separate review and a later change. Legal points are research-based judgments, not legal advice. The fee stays a fixed introduction fee by job size; no ranking, recommending or vetting; pest control never advertised; ROC and city lists verification-only; no marketing texts; Gate P and build_hold unchanged.

- **D-270** Question (homeowner v7 #1, trace S5): after a homeowner chooses emergency at night, every call window is 8 a.m. to 8 p.m., the Confirm and card-fix timers count business hours, and the missed-call text waits for morning. Choice: for a job chosen as an emergency, PICK-018 offers "Now (any hour)" first and as the default; PICK-044: its Confirm window and its card-fix window run in clock time, 60 minutes each (settings), at any hour, the homeowner is told "If they don't confirm by <time>, you can choose another quote.", its quote cards show "Takes after-hours calls" for businesses that ticked BIZ-007, and a pick at night of a business that didn't tick it tells the homeowner that business sees it at 8 a.m.; QH-004 allows the who's-calling and missed-call texts on emergency jobs at any hour; the release says "Call now." when the window is Now. Reason: the changes file offers an emergency path and after-hours businesses (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:86`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:87`); NWS: heat stroke needs 911 at once (plan R-N1).
- **D-271** Question (trace S4): nothing tells a homeowner at night that no business in the trade takes after-hours work. Choice: INT-033: between 8 p.m. and 8 a.m., before the INT-006 choice, the homeowner gets "Ask Hava: <N> <trade> businesses on Ask Hava take after-hours calls."; with N of 0 the choice is not offered and the text says "Ask Hava: No <trade> business on Ask Hava takes after-hours calls yet. Your request goes to businesses at 8 a.m. If anyone feels unwell or it isn't safe, call 911."; a night emergency job with no quote 60 minutes (a setting) after its cards went gets one text saying no after-hours business has quoted yet and the request also goes to every <trade> business at 8 a.m., which it does. Reason: a firm, honest expectation beats silence (homeowner v7 #1d).
- **D-272** Question (homeowner v7 #5, trace S8): the carbon-monoxide text says "leave now" only "if you smell gas", and CO has no smell. Choice: INT-004: a CO match (carbon monoxide, co alarm, co detector, monóxido) or a symptom keyword (dizzy, head hurts, nausea, mareo, náusea) puts first "Carbon monoxide has no smell. If a CO alarm is sounding, or anyone has a headache, dizziness, nausea or confusion, get everyone outside to fresh air now and call 911 from outside. Don't go back in until responders say it's safe.", then "Once everyone is outside, call your gas company too. Keep the appliance off until a licensed pro checks it."; a gas match adds "Don't flip switches or light anything."; INT-002 adds co detector, monóxido, nausea and náusea; the generic gas line stays. Reason: CPSC: CO is colorless and odorless; on an alarm move outside, call 911 from outside, don't go back in until responders allow it (plan R-C1); UniSource gas safety: no switches or flames near a leak, call 911 and the utility from a safe place (plan R-G1); the generic line stays (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:196`).
- **D-273** Question (homeowner v7 safety items 4 and 5): electrical and water matches get only the generic gas line. Choice: INT-034: an electrical match adds "If you see smoke or flames, get everyone out and call 911 from outside. Don't touch a sparking or hot outlet, cord or panel; stop using it and call a licensed electrician."; a water match (flood, inund) adds "Stay out of standing water near outlets, cords or appliances. Don't stand in water to reach the breaker panel. If you can do it safely, shut off the main water valve."; both keep the generic line and are marked for the same desk check as the heat text. Reason: NFPA tip sheet (plan R-E1); electric-utility flood guidance (plan R-W1); the generic line stays (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:196`).
- **D-274** Question (trace S9; safety item 4; owner G8): the spec doesn't say whether the heat text goes before consent, common AC-failure words miss the heat match, and a heat message during a texting suspension raises no alert. Choice: CON-001 and CON-027 list the heat-safety text (INT-031) with the safety text as sent first, the consent text right after; INT-031's heat words add warm, not cold, stopped, died, won't turn on and broke next to an AC or cooler word; TXT-024 treats a heat match like an emergency keyword for the critical alert and the email and web-chat safety text. Reason: the safety text goes before the consent text (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:86`); D-208.
- **D-275** Question (ChatGPT research Part 2): the automated safety voice call (D-206, D-210) relies on consent the research calls uncertain for a prerecorded call to a mobile. Choice: TXT-024 and CALL-019 keep the design behind a "safety voice call" setting that is off by default and can be switched on only with a TCPA attorney's written approval recorded in the owner portal (date and reference); while off, OPS-028 pushes every critical alert as before. Reason: prior express consent for prerecorded calls (47 U.S.C. 227(b)(1)(A), 47 CFR 64.1200, as cited in the research); legal fit outranks automation.
- **D-276** Question (homeowner v7 #16): the picked business isn't told a safety text was sent. Choice: CARD-007 adds the fixed field "Safety text sent: <gas smell / carbon monoxide / electrical / water / heat>", never free text. Reason: fixed card fields only (D-202).
- **D-277** Question (owner v7 3.1, G3 to G6, G9): urgent call-backs, safety-word complaints, "Twilio restored" and a failing license list wait for a window and miss their targets. Choice: OPS-032: at 9 a.m. and 3 p.m. Arizona time, when any are open, one push lists urgent call-backs, urgent HELP messages, safety-word complaints, a Twilio "restored" notice and a license list still failing after 24 hours; it never breaks Do Not Disturb and nothing is sent when nothing is open; OPS-029 names it; INT-023 counts a request with an emergency keyword as urgent before "How soon?" is answered. Reason: the longest wait is 18 hours, inside the 24-hour promise; owner sim about 0.17 extra interruptions a week normally [sim].
- **D-278** Question (owner G1, G2): safe mode starts from High alerts that never reach Casey's phone. Choice: OPS-001: safe mode starts only when a High alert that pushed under OPS-029 or OPS-032 is unacknowledged 24 hours after its push; OPS-013: a failed test restore is listed in the digest as due at the next window and never starts safe mode; OPS-023: a delivery alert never starts safe mode; the ledger's clock starts at its push. Reason: safe mode guards money while Casey is unreachable (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:73`); 38 hours of needless safe mode per 24 months [sim].
- **D-279** Question (owner G10, G12): the 4-day targets hold only if the windows are at most 4 days apart, and nothing says the host restarts the app. Choice: OPS-033: the owner portal schedules the weekly window and mid-week check no more than 4 days apart and lists what falls due before the next one; the host restarts the app on a failed health check, and the 15-minute push says to press Restart in the host dashboard. Reason: owner sim calendar check (max gap 4.08 days).
- **D-280** Question (owner cut #2): every complaint needs a decision. Choice: OPS-005 and BIZ-012: a complaint is a digest line needing no decision unless the business has 2 or more complaints in 90 days or a safety word is present; the 3-in-90 pause is unchanged. Reason: about 2.5 to 6 minutes a week saved [sim]; the automatic pause still protects homeowners (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:91`).
- **D-281** Question (owner cut #5): letter-path sign-ups are flagged even after the letter's code is entered. Choice: BIZ-016: the letter path is no longer a flag once the code from the letter is entered; mismatches and fraud signals still flag. Reason: the letter goes only to the public-record address, the same proof as a code to the record phone (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:37`); up to 26 minutes a week in a crowded launch [sim].
- **D-282** Question (owner 1.7, G6): LIC-039 claims live lookups keep statuses current while a list fails, but they use the same page reader. Choice: LIC-039 says the live lookup helps when the download fails, not when the page layout changes, and a list still failing after 24 hours joins the OPS-032 push, before LIC-008's 72-hour pause. Reason: correct the claim; no new spend.
- **D-283** Question (business v7 4.1; homeowner v7 #8a, #8b): five required quote-fact taps on every quote, no text path for them, and the facts miss the visit time and whether the price may change. Choice: PICK-040: the quote form pre-fills every fact except earliest visit from the business's last answers for that job type and a "Same as last time" tap confirms them; earliest visit is a date plus morning, afternoon, evening or tonight; a new fact "price may change after seeing the job: yes / no"; a "Free estimate" definition; PICK-006: a text quote supplies its facts on the read-back screen it already passes through. Reason: quote facts stay the business's own statements (D-233); the read-back already exists (D-149).
- **D-284** Question (business v7 4.2, X04): HOLD isn't named in the pick text, its limit on urgent jobs is unclear, and lapsed picks are invisible. Choice: PICK-037: every non-emergency pick text and reminder ends "Reply HOLD <n> for 2 more hours."; PICK-043: HOLD is refused only when the homeowner chose emergency (INT-006), never for a "Today" answer alone; BIZ-025 counts lapsed picks; OPS-034 flags for the monthly review, with no automatic action, a business with 3 or more lapsed picks in 30 days and a phone with 3 or more requests in 30 days that never picked. Reason: D-252; no ranking effect.
- **D-285** Question (business v7 4.2(3)): businesses out of signal miss the Confirm window even with HOLD. Choice: BIZ-034: the owner or a "Confirm and billing" phone can set "Away until <time>", up to 4 business hours ahead, in the portal or by AWAY <hours> (AUSENTE <hours>); a non-emergency pick arriving while away starts its Confirm clock when the away time ends, the homeowner is told "Ask Hava: <Business> is on a job until <time>. You'll hear by <time>. You can still choose another quote on your page."; Away never applies to emergency jobs and a pick delayed by Away can't be HOLDed. Reason: same bounded-wait pattern as HOLD (D-252).
- **D-286** Question (business v7 4.4): the call window decides refunds but the business sees it only after paying; "went out" is outside its control; a bare YES cancels an email-job refund; no call log. Choice: PICK-021 and PICK-037 show the homeowner's contact choice and call window and "Refund if you can't reach them: 2 call attempts at least 3 hours apart, inside their window, within 72 hours. They can be on different days."; FEE-022: the missed-call text "was sent or attempted by Ask Hava", and a delivery failure never counts against the business; JOB-027 asks "Did <business> email you and agree a time? Reply YES or NO."; BIZ-035: a per-job call log on the portal job page. Reason: a window is not contact information (PICK-022 releases only contact details); FEE-022's data already exists (D-235, D-236, D-243).
- **D-287** Question (business v7 4.6): a charge question gets a generic robot reply. Choice: BIZ-032: a business text with charge, fee, refund or "$" gets the latest charge's job number, amount, tier and its "Why was I charged?" link, then the 4-day line; TERMS-022 adds "If a charge was our mistake, it is refunded in full." Reason: D-225, D-228.
- **D-288** Question (business v7 4.3, 4.5, 4.7, 4.8, 4.9): smaller frictions. Choice: BIZ-030 adds "If many picks in your trade end without a hire, Casey reviews it and may add a credit."; BIZ-021 says "No priority; everyone waiting is told at once."; FEE-040's lock for a business waiting on its city-list match starts no earlier than its activation; BIZ-022 also refuses directions to other channels ("find us on", search, Google, Facebook, Yelp); BIZ-024 adds "Ask the owner to read you the 6 digits, or forward the text."; BIZ-026 shows the typical list delay (a setting); CALL-012 and BIZ-033: a phone linked by a non-owner works only after the owner replies YES. Reason: business v7 4.3 to 4.9.
- **D-289** Question (homeowner v7 #2, owner G7): "reply HELP to reach a person" has no time attached and can loop to the recording. Choice: CON-031 reads "Our texts are automated. Reply HELP for help. A person reads HELP messages within 4 days, or within 24 hours if your job is urgent."; CON-018: the HELP reply names the help email and askhava.com/help, says the Ask Hava number is text-only and gives no voice number unless a separate help line exists, and the help-phone setting may never be the texting number; the next message after a HELP is queued for Casey with that due date (urgent ones in OPS-032); help emails get an automatic reply with the same targets and are listed in the digest; WEB-008 and WEB-021 say the same. Reason: WORD-009 truthful answers (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:197`); Casey's windows (D-218).
- **D-290** Question (homeowner v7 #3, #13): nobody learns LIST exists; Spanish speakers must reply in English. Choice: TXT-016's first-quote text ends "Can't open links? Reply LIST to get your quotes by text." (Spanish "Responda LISTA"), the HELP reply and quote page say the same; TXT-031's Spanish ending reads "responda el número y SÍ, por ejemplo 2 SÍ"; PICK-015 accepts "<n> SÍ" and "<n> SI". Reason: D-239; every text kind has a Spanish template (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:88`).
- **D-291** Question (homeowner v7 #4): a text pick never asks the call window. Choice: PICK-031: after a "<n> YES" pick, one text asks "Ask Hava: When can they call? 1) Any time 8-8 2) Mornings 3) 12-1 4) After 5" with "0) Now" on emergency jobs, asked before the PICK-025 question; no reply in 15 minutes means any time 8 to 8 (Now on an emergency job); on non-emergency jobs the business's pick text waits for the answer or the 15 minutes, on emergency jobs it goes at once. Reason: D-235.
- **D-292** Question (homeowner v7 #7): "How soon?" shows the after-hours cost warning in daytime, has no needed-by date, and "Today" changes nothing visible. Choice: INT-006's wording depends on the time of day; INT-030 adds "4) By a certain day", which asks the day and puts "Needed by <day>" on the card (CARD-007); TXT-032 for a "Today" job adds "You can choose as soon as the first quote arrives. If it can't wait, it's fine to call a company you know too." Reason: D-208.
- **D-293** Question (homeowner v7 #9, #18): businesses aren't told to call only inside the window, and times show in Arizona time only. Choice: the release and TXT-026 say "Call only inside the homeowner's window."; TXT-033: every time in a homeowner text says "Arizona time" and adds the area-code zone's time when it differs; TXT-030 adds "Reply TIMES to change when they call.", and TIMES (HORARIO) asks the PICK-031 window question again. Reason: QH-003 already derives the area-code zone.
- **D-294** Question (homeowner v7 #10): the on-site person can't be the one called. Choice: PICK-025 option 2 adds "Should the business call them first? Reply FIRST." (PRIMERO), passed with the release; after the who's-calling text the homeowner gets one text to forward, "Ask Hava: <Business> will contact <name> about the repair. Ask for the price in writing. Don't pay in full up front."; the on-site contact is still never texted (CON-021). Reason: D-212, D-213.
- **D-295** Question (homeowner v7 #14, #17, #19 to #24): smaller items. Choice: JOB-015's check-in first line is "Reply DONE, PROBLEM, or NO if they never reached you. Not done yet? Reply LATER. More answers: reply MORE." and the day-3 check counts from the later of the release and the quote's earliest visit date; TXT-016 and TXT-031 label recurring amounts "per month"; PICK-041's line appears once in full at the top of the quote page and as one short sentence on every card; WEB-022 adds "Texts run automatically day and night. Safety texts go out at any hour."; PICK-017's review step says "You're choosing one business for this request. If it falls through, you can choose another quote." and shows "or reply <n> YES"; CON-018 and the pick contact step say Canadian numbers may pay roaming; CALL-018: an email-contact release says "Homeowner chose email. Email first; calls may not be heard." Reason: homeowner v7 items.
- **D-296** Question (ChatGPT research Part 3 §4): Twilio Proxy is a Public Beta with no SLA, which a safety-sensitive call path shouldn't depend on. Choice: CALL-021: masked calls are built on the provider's standard programmable voice (call webhooks and dialing out with the pool number as caller ID), never on a beta or no-SLA masking product such as Twilio Proxy; `docs/architecture.md` section 7 describes the voice adapter in provider-neutral terms. Reason: research cites the Proxy page; BUILD_BRIEF's stack is unchanged.
- **D-297** Question (ChatGPT research Part 1, combined list item 2): web-and-email mode is written as an outage fallback, not a way to run the whole service. Choice: TXT-021 and TXT-034: every homeowner and business step can be completed by web chat, the quote page, the portal and email alone, a launch-channel setting can start the service in that mode, and every end-to-end journey has a test that runs with zero texts sent; WEB-009: web chat is always available, not only when texting is down. Reason: the changes file's fallback runs "web-and-email only" (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:177`); which messages go by text is unchanged.
- **D-298** Question (ChatGPT research Part 2 §1): an FCC consent-revocation order is reported effective Nov 25, 2026. Choice: CON-032: before Gate C a build-time check confirms the opt-out rules meet it (7 standard words conclusive, any reasonable method, processed at once and never later than 10 business days, one opt-out covers all Ask Hava texts and automated calls, one confirmation within 5 minutes with no marketing, at least STOP disclosed), and the rule's text and effective date are re-checked and logged; the date is as reported by research and must be verified. Reason: FCC 26-67 per the research; corroborated in part (order released Oct 1, 2026, effective 30 days after Federal Register publication, plan R-L3).
- **D-299** Question (ChatGPT research Part 1 §2): the privacy wording must state the anonymous job-card sharing and the post-pick disclosure exactly. Choice: TERMS-025: the privacy policy and the homeowner data page say "When you send a request, Ask Hava sends a description of the job, with your name, phone number, email and street address removed, to local businesses that do that kind of work. Only after you pick a business, and it confirms and pays its introduction fee, does Ask Hava give that one business your first name and the job address, plus the on-site contact and email if you chose to share them. Ask Hava never gives your phone number to any business, and never sells or shares phone numbers or texting permission for marketing."; TERMS-023 points to it. Reason: truthful disclosure (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:176`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:191`).
- **D-300** Question (Grok research): suggested pitch words ("pre-vetted", "concierge", "exclusive local intros", "pay-per-lead") break the wording rules. Choice: WORD-020: no template, page or document for homeowners or businesses says "lead" or "leads" (except "lead paint", "lead pipe" and "lead-based"), "pre-vetted" or "exclusive"; the only exclusivity claim is "Only the business you pick gets your contact info."; WORD-012's example drops "lead"; `scripts/check_catalog.py` bans the same words in the catalog. Reason: WORD-003, WORD-019; never "vetted" (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:194`).
- **D-301** Question (plan section 3): which proposals are rejected or deferred. Choice: not adopted now: refusing uncovered categories before the consent text (the changes file requires consent first, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:113`), a PROBLEM consequence (Casey's call), category-specific quote facts (after interview C22), tap-to-ask (D-269), a 3-business-hour default Confirm window (Away instead), OCUPADO from a "Cards and quotes" phone (D-259), "text me first" (CALL-016), changing the immigration line before testing, storing a hash of the phone number (lawyer list), a 2-month spot check, pre-approved reader-only deploys (OPS-022), relaxing the urgent call-back promise, the "concierge", "pre-vetted" and "exclusive" pitch lines, changing the pilot trades (Casey's call), and any change to which messages go by text, the campaign wording or toll-free (separate review). Reason: each conflicts with a higher priority, a hard rule or a source line, or is Casey's decision.
- **D-302** Question (ChatGPT and Grok research): legal, tax, payment, insurance, company-setup and name items need Casey, not a spec change. Choice: listed in `docs/for-casey-review.md`: Twilio's written eligibility answer, a TCPA attorney, CPA or ADOR on TPT (the fee may not be taxable; no license on assumption), Stripe's written clearance, insurance quotes (about $1,300 to $5,450 a year, an estimate), LLC address limits and agent county, the city license ($196, verify), FinCEN BOI (not required for a domestic LLC, verify), the trademark attorney and local "Hava ___" businesses, and the pilot trades. Reason: BUILD_BRIEF §16 (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:542`).

**Spec changes from fix round 8:** `businesses.md` BIZ-012, BIZ-016, BIZ-021, BIZ-022, BIZ-024, BIZ-025, BIZ-026, BIZ-030, BIZ-032, BIZ-033 changed; BIZ-034, BIZ-035 added; `calls.md` CALL-012, CALL-018, CALL-019 changed; CALL-021 added; `consent.md` CON-001, CON-018, CON-027, CON-031 changed; CON-032 added; `fees.md` FEE-020, FEE-022, FEE-040 changed; `intake.md` INT-002, INT-004, INT-006, INT-023, INT-030, INT-031 changed; INT-033, INT-034 added; `job-cards.md` CARD-007 changed; `licensing.md` LIC-039 changed; `lifecycle.md` JOB-013, JOB-014, JOB-015, JOB-016, JOB-027 changed; `messaging.md` TXT-016, TXT-021, TXT-024, TXT-026, TXT-027, TXT-030, TXT-031, TXT-032 changed; TXT-033, TXT-034 added; `ops-recovery.md` OPS-001, OPS-005, OPS-013, OPS-023, OPS-028, OPS-029, OPS-030 changed; OPS-032, OPS-033, OPS-034 added; `quiet-hours.md` QH-004 changed; `quotes-pick.md` PICK-006, PICK-015, PICK-017, PICK-018, PICK-019, PICK-021, PICK-025, PICK-031, PICK-037, PICK-040, PICK-041, PICK-043 changed; PICK-044 added; `terms.md` TERMS-022, TERMS-023 changed; TERMS-025 added; `website.md` WEB-008, WEB-009, WEB-021, WEB-022 changed; `wording.md` WORD-012 changed; WORD-020 added.
