## Messaging Option A, October 28, 2026

Casey, Oct 28, 2026: "Ok fix everything we need to. If Twilio is going to be a problem look for other options." and then, at 06:22, "we also want email as an option. I'd like the business to choose email, text or both, but if text isn't possible we can do email and then later maybe an app." Input, outside this repository: the messaging risk research `/home/claude/hhq/outputs/messaging_options/Messaging_Risk_and_Options.md` (desk research, Oct 28, 2026; not legal advice), which recommends Option A, "split by audience" (its sections 1, 3, 6 and 7). Its findings: every US texting provider sends 10-digit texting through the same Campaign Registry and carrier review, so switching providers doesn't remove the risk (Telnyx publishes the same rejection code 708, "Lead Gen/Affiliate Marketing Prohibited"); texts between Ask Hava and a homeowner about their own request are low to moderate risk; job-card, pick and reminder texts to businesses are moderate to high risk under Twilio error 30951 ("operates as an affiliate or intermediary, capturing consumer interest for products or services offered by unrelated third parties"; a rejected campaign "is not eligible for resubmission"); the details text carrying the homeowner's first name and address is the riskiest single message; and the drafted campaign description says "businesses never send messages on this campaign" while the spec texted businesses from the same number. The research put an installable web app with push first for businesses; Casey's 06:22 change makes email the launch channel, lets each business choose email, text or both once business texting is approved, and moves the app to a later, optional phase. The research's placeholder IDs TXT-033, TXT-034, BIZ-034 and CON-032 were already used by fix round 8, so the new rules are TXT-035 onward, BIZ-036 onward and CON-033; its "CALL-017 (add)" is already CALL-021 (D-296). Gate P and build_hold are unchanged.

- **D-303** Question (research sections 1, 3, 6): which messages go by text? Choice: Option A. TXT-035: each audience has its own channel setting: homeowners SMS (default) or web-and-email; for businesses a business-texting switch, off by default, that can be turned on only after Casey files the texting provider's written eligibility answer in the owner portal (date, reference and the answer itself) and a separate business campaign on its own number is registered; TXT-021 and TXT-022 apply per audience; TXT-037: for every business whose channel is email (all of them while the switch is off), no text goes to its phones except Verify codes and the STOP, HELP and START replies carriers require, every notice goes by email with the same words, every keyword and text quote is replaced by a secure portal link, and a text from a business phone is never read as a homeowner request or a command and gets no other text reply. Reason: the research rates homeowner texts as first-party customer care (CTIA Exhibit II: "If the Consumer initiates the conversation and the Non-Consumer simply responds, then no additional permission is expected") and business job texts as the risk that can sink the brand; the design works whichever way Twilio answers; the changes file's fallback already runs without texts (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:177`).
- **D-304** Question (research section 1, "a problem I found"): the drafted campaign description says businesses send no messages on the campaign while the spec texted job cards, picks and reminders to businesses and read their replies on the same number, and carriers require a campaign to describe all its traffic. Choice: TERMS-011: the homeowner campaign describes only Ask Hava-homeowner texts and adds "Businesses get no texts on this number; they are notified by email."; TERMS-013: its samples are homeowner templates only and match the traffic exactly, a test failing if any business template could be sent from the homeowner number; business texting, if ever approved, gets its own campaign, number, description and samples (a job card, a pick notice and the STOP reply). Reason: the drafted wording (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:179`) becomes true instead of incomplete; samples are real templates (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:175`).
- **D-305** Question (research sections 1 and 8): D-088 chose "no pre-clearance request". Choice: reversed. TERMS-014: the account checklist has Casey send the written eligibility question to Twilio (draft in `docs/for-casey-review.md`, signed "Casey Solomon, Ask Hava (LLC being formed)" until the LLC exists) and wait for the written answer before registering; the homeowner campaign is registered even if the business answer is no; the Telnyx question is sent only if Twilio's answer is unclear; nothing is sent, signed up for or registered without Casey (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:61`). Reason: v9.3 already said to "describe the model to Twilio support and get their read before registering" (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:253`); a 30951 refusal can't be resubmitted, so asking first costs only days.
- **D-306** Question (research sections 3 and 7, PICK-024): the details text sends the homeowner's first name and address to a business by SMS. Choice: TXT-036: no text to anyone other than the homeowner ever carries a homeowner's name, street address, email, on-site contact or a photo link, on any number and whatever the business chose; PICK-024: the release goes to the business portal and by email only, and a text (for a business that chose text) says only that the details are ready. Reason: consumer details texted to another business is the plain shape of carrier code 708 and Twilio 30951.
- **D-307** Question (Casey, 06:22: "we also want email as an option. I'd like the business to choose email, text or both, but if text isn't possible we can do email and then later maybe an app."): how do businesses choose their channel? Choice: BIZ-036: each business has a channel preference for job cards, picks and reminders, set at sign-up and changeable in the portal: email (the default and the only choice while the business-texting switch is off), text, or both, with text and both offered only while the switch is on; choosing text later shows the unchecked text-consent box (CON-019); BIZ-041: the installable business web app with push notifications is a later, optional phase that is not built before launch and blocks no gate, and when a later spec change adds it, "push" joins the same preference, with the research's iPhone Home Screen steps (iOS 16.4 or newer), permission only from a tap, a test notification and email as the fallback; until then the portal registers no service worker and WEB-001 is unchanged; WEB-024: the business page and sign-up say how notices arrive, following the setting. Reason: Casey's words; email needs no carrier registration; the research's app-first design (its section 6) is kept as the later phase so nothing is lost.
- **D-308** Question (research section 7, BIZ-019, CARD-001, CARD-018): what does "the business gets a notice" mean now? Choice: BIZ-019: every business notice goes by the business's chosen channels (BIZ-036), email by default, and is shown in the portal, and a homeowner texting switch or suspension never changes it; BIZ-037: job cards go to the owner and every "Cards and quotes" member, picks, Confirm reminders and money notices to the owner and every "Confirm and billing" member, account notices to the owner, each person by their own sign-in email (and, when the business chose text, their linked phone with text consent); CARD-020: a card's email (subject "Ask Hava: New <category> job in <town>. Quote by <time>.") carries the full card and a secure Quote link, and its 24-hour window counts from when the notice was sent; QH-005: every channel of one business notice, email included, is sent or held by quiet hours together, so each clock has one start; TXT-014, TXT-026, PICK-026, PICK-035, FEE-020 send by the business's channels. Reason: research section 7 ("'a job card goes' means ... email"); quiet hours apply to businesses alike (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:184`); the card window starts when the card is actually sent (D-042).
- **D-309** Question (the Option A brief: HOLD, PAUSE/BUSY, YES <n>, CONFIRM, quote by text, Away until must each work by email): Choice: BIZ-038: each is a portal page that works without JavaScript, one tap from a secure link in the matching email: Quote (the quote form), Confirm (the PICK-021 review step), "Hold for 2 more hours", "Away until", "Pause job cards" and "Resume", "Booked for <n> days" (1 to 60), "Link this phone", "Approve" a member-linked phone and "Ask about a charge", with the same role and timing rules; Confirm and every pause, booking, link or approval needs a signed-in session, while the Hold link in a pick email may act through a signed single-purpose link (once, that pick, that person, until the window ends); an email reply never performs any action: a reply is read only as a question, answered automatically with the portal link and, with a question or charge word, sent to Casey as BIZ-032 says, because email senders can be forged; PICK-006, PICK-033, PICK-043, BIZ-028, BIZ-032, BIZ-034: the keywords and text quotes work only for a business that chose text or both. Reason: the brief's "secure links to the portal; email replies only where safe"; a forwarded Hold link can only add 2 hours once, so it needs no session, while money and account changes do (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:315`, `BUILD_BRIEF.md:406`).
- **D-310** Question (research sections 6 and 7, PICK-019, PICK-037): the 2-hour Confirm window counted from delivery of the pick text, and email alone is easy to miss on a ladder. Choice: BIZ-039: each pick notice and its 60-minute reminder place one automated call to the owner's phone and each "Confirm and billing" phone with pick calls on: "This is Ask Hava LLC. You were picked for job <n>. Check your email or askhava.com to confirm by <time>. Press 1 to hear this again. Press 2 for 2 more hours." with the callback number, spoken slowly, never to an opted-out phone, naming no homeowner, address, amount or fee; press 2 is a HOLD with PICK-043's limits; a machine gets the message without the options; PICK-019 and PICK-044: the window counts from when the pick notice is sent (email, call and any text together); PICK-037: the notice is an email plus the call (plus a text when chosen), with a Hold link on non-emergency jobs; PICK-018 and PICK-031 say "pick notice". Reason: a call is harder to miss than a message (research section 6, an estimate); business sign-up consent already covers automated calls (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:181`); the identity-call opening and callback number are reused (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:182`); press 1 to repeat as for the code call (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:417`).
- **D-311** Question (the Option A brief: team roles, owner alerts on staff confirm, STOP from staff phones): Choice: BIZ-033: the owner gets an email (and a text when chosen) when a non-owner confirms or links a phone, and a member-linked phone works only after the owner taps Approve from that email (or replies YES by text when chosen); CALL-011: a blocked staff call sends the owner and "Confirm and billing" members an email with a "Link this phone" link; CALL-012 follows; BIZ-017: a STOP texted from the owner's phone stops texts and automated calls to it; the business stays active on email (a text-only business is moved to email) and the owner is emailed that pick calls are off and that START turns them back on; a team phone's STOP affects only that phone; the portal's pick-call and card-email switches are never an opt-out of anything else; BIZ-018: language is per person. Reason: roles and per-phone consent stay as D-191 set them; one opt-out covers texts and automated calls (CON-032, D-298); pausing a whole business because one channel stopped would punish it for a channel it doesn't need.
- **D-312** Question (research section 7, CON-019, CON-020; the brief: verified business email; BIZ-020, BIZ-021, BIZ-026, BIZ-031 text businesses outside picks): Choice: BIZ-040: the sign-up email is verified before activation by a single-use link or 6-digit code sent to it, a hard bounce or spam complaint marks it unverified with a portal banner and an email to the owner's other team addresses, and a changed notice email must be verified before notices move to it, the old address being told; CON-019: the checkbox wording names each channel (email for job notices, automated calls about picks, and texts only if the business chooses them once business texting is on), and choosing text later shows the same unchecked box for texts; CON-020: the welcome is an email, with the welcome text only for a business that chose text, and still no card before it; BIZ-020, BIZ-021, BIZ-026, BIZ-031: status, waitlist, updated-record and trade-open notices go by email, by text only when chosen. Reason: email is now the main channel, so it must be proven; business consent wording names what is sent (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:181`); the first message to anyone names the brand and HELP and STOP (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:257`).
- **D-313** Question (research section 7, BIZ-002, SEC-004, PICK-013): do one-time codes depend on the campaign? Choice: TXT-038: every pick, identity and sign-in code is sent only through Twilio Verify (by SMS or voice), never through the outbox on a campaign number; BIZ-002 and SEC-004 say so. Reason: Twilio's A2P 10DLC overview: "If you're only using 10DLC numbers to send user verification text messages, you can use Twilio Verify rather than registering for A2P 10DLC" (research section 2); codes keep working if a campaign is refused.
- **D-314** Question (found while applying Option A): Casey's High and critical alerts were texts to his phone (QH-004; `sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:457`), which would be traffic on the homeowner campaign that its description doesn't cover. Choice: OPS-035: every alert that OPS-029 or OPS-032 says pushes to Casey's phone is sent as an email to his alert address, which the owner guide sets to notify on his phone, never as a text from a campaign number; a critical safety alert also places one automated call to his phone from the Ask Hava number, and the owner guide tells him to let that number through Do Not Disturb, so the break-through promise (OPS-029) still holds; QH-004 no longer lists alerts to Casey. Reason: the campaign must describe all its traffic (D-304); a call from an allowed caller breaks Do Not Disturb; the safety path stays intact.
- **D-315** Question (research sections 6 and 7, new CON-032): what if the 10DLC homeowner campaign is refused? Choice: CON-033: with Casey's go, toll-free verification for the homeowner stream with the same content and samples, never for business notices without written approval, and only after a refused registration, never to get around a suspension (TXT-022 unchanged); web-and-email mode stays the fallback meanwhile (TXT-021). Reason: Twilio error 30468 lets a toll-free rejection be corrected and resubmitted, unlike 30951 (research section 2); "no second number" is about suspensions (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:267`).
- **D-316** Question (research section 7, TERMS-008 and site wording): Choice: WORD-020 also bans "affiliate" (except inside the TERMS-009 sentence) and "partner" or "partners"; WEB-024 (D-307): by default "Job requests come by email, and we call you when a homeowner picks you. We don't text businesses."; TERMS-021: the business data page lists the notice email and channel choice and the pick-call log. TERMS-025's sharing sentence (D-299) is unchanged. Reason: Telnyx code 708: "Any mention of lead generation or SEO on the website would lead to a decline" and Twilio's forbidden "Affiliate marketing" row (research section 2); the TERMS-009 sentence is required as written (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:180`).
- **D-317** Question (the Option A brief: accessibility must cover what businesses use instead of texts): Choice: A11Y-023: the channel-preference settings, every BIZ-038 action page and the emails that link to them meet A11Y-001 to A11Y-019: link text names the action and the job ("Confirm job 1042"), never "click here", each email has a plain-text part, the Confirm window shows as an end time, never a countdown, and every action reached from an email shows what it will do before it does it, except the single Hold; A11Y-024: pick calls speak slowly and repeat (press 1), each call's content also goes by email, and a person can switch pick calls off (for example a deaf owner using relay) without losing any notice; A11Y-022: the Gate B VoiceOver script adds opening a pick email and confirming or holding from it; the later app phase (BIZ-041) carries its own accessibility rules. Reason: WCAG 2.2 AA for every portal (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:384`); emails (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:419`); the voice-call rule (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:417`); error prevention for money (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:406`).
- **D-318** Question (the Option A brief: email deliverability, verified business email, threat model for email and later push): Choice: SEC-025: business and owner emails come from askhava.com through the planned email provider (Resend, behind the `email` adapter) with SPF, DKIM and DMARC at p=reject, a separate sending subdomain, bounce and complaint webhooks handled (BIZ-040), never ask for card details or codes, link only to askhava.com, and every action link is single-purpose, signed, bound to one person and one object, used once and expires; SEC-024: a business's notice addresses change only from a signed-in owner session, the new address must be verified and the old one is told, and action links stop working for a removed member; BIZ-041 lists the push rules for the later phase (subscriptions only from a signed-in session, a push-service allowlist, VAPID key as a secret, no homeowner details, a service worker that caches no job page); `docs/threat-model.md` adds the email rows now and notes push for later. Reason: ASVS L2 (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:298`); secrets never in code (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:62`).
- **D-319** Question (research section 7, OPS-004): Choice: OPS-004 counts text delivery per audience and per number and adds alerts when more than 5% of the last 100 business emails hard-bounce or are refused or fewer than 80% of the last 20 pick calls are placed; OPS-008 shows email and pick-call rates; these alerts wait in the portal and digest and never push (OPS-029 unchanged). Reason: research section 7 (its push alert becomes an email alert); Casey's time.
- **D-320** Question: which tasks build this? Choice: two new Stage 4 build tasks: T147 (business notices by chosen channel, the channel preference, email verification and the email-deliverability rules) and T148 (secure portal action links for every former keyword and the automated pick call), each before the J2 journey (T099) and the Stage 4 clean-up (T100); changed requirements stay with the tasks that already own them, and OPS-035 goes to T105 and T108; no task builds the later app phase (BIZ-041), and T147 only keeps the channel list open for it. Reason: small PRs (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:485`); the app is post-launch by Casey's 06:22 change.
- **D-321** Question (research sections 4 to 6): which options are not adopted? Choice: not adopted now: the installable app with push at launch (later, BIZ-041), switching texting providers (same registry and carrier rules), a short code (cost, same review), RCS (same review plus more), WhatsApp (weak reach for older homeowners), Apple Messages for Business (can't start a conversation), a native app (weeks of build), Option B (one campaign for both audiences) and Option C (no homeowner texting) except as the existing fallback; Plivo's masking API is noted as the fallback if Twilio ever closes the voice account. Reason: research section 6 ranking; Casey's 06:22 change.
- **D-322** Question: what needs Casey? Choice: listed first in `docs/for-casey-review.md`: send the Twilio question (and Telnyx only if Twilio's answer is unclear), signed "Casey Solomon, Ask Hava (LLC being formed)" until the LLC exists; ask the TCPA attorney to confirm the business consent wording covers prerecorded pick calls; set up the askhava.com email records (SPF, DKIM, DMARC) with the email provider at the account checkpoint; decide later whether to turn business texting on and whether to build the app. Reason: BUILD_BRIEF §16 (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:542`); no outreach without Casey (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:61`).

**Spec changes from Messaging Option A:** `messaging.md` TXT-014, TXT-021, TXT-022, TXT-026, TXT-034 changed, TXT-035 to TXT-038 added; `businesses.md` BIZ-002, BIZ-017 to BIZ-021, BIZ-026, BIZ-028, BIZ-031 to BIZ-034 changed, BIZ-036 to BIZ-041 added; `quotes-pick.md` PICK-006, PICK-018, PICK-019, PICK-024, PICK-026, PICK-031, PICK-033, PICK-035, PICK-037, PICK-043, PICK-044 changed; `job-cards.md` CARD-020 added; `consent.md` CON-019, CON-020 changed, CON-033 added; `calls.md` CALL-011, CALL-012 changed; `quiet-hours.md` QH-004, QH-005 changed; `fees.md` FEE-020 changed; `terms.md` TERMS-011, TERMS-013, TERMS-014, TERMS-021 changed; `website.md` WEB-024 added; `wording.md` WORD-020 changed; `accessibility.md` A11Y-022 changed, A11Y-023, A11Y-024 added; `security.md` SEC-004 changed, SEC-024, SEC-025 added; `ops-recovery.md` OPS-004, OPS-008 changed, OPS-035 added.

## Fix round 9, October 28, 2026

Casey, Oct 28, 2026: "Ok fix everything we need to." and "do whatever research you need to to make the most ideal changes." Inputs, all outside this repository: the sim_v9 homeowner and business findings (`/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v9/homeowner/Homeowner_Angle_Findings_v9.md` with its `08_safety_trace.md`, and `/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v9/business/Business_Angle_Findings_v9.md`; AI-simulated people, not real ones; [sim] numbers are simulated), the messaging research (`/home/claude/hhq/outputs/messaging_options/Messaging_Risk_and_Options.md`) and new desk research cited in the plan `/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v9/Fix_Round_9_Plan.md` section 6 (PHMSA leak signs; Santa Cruz County's Spanish gas-leak sheet; CPSC's Spanish CO page; CDC's Spanish CO symptoms; the USFA smoke-alarm sheet; Kidde on chirps; 47 CFR 64.1200; Gmail and Outlook.com spam help; Postmark on Apple Mail Privacy Protection; a Supabase discussion on scanners using up one-time links). Not legal advice. The owner-angle round-9 simulation was still running and is not used. Priorities: safety, then the business email-first frictions, then homeowners. Business texting stays off until Twilio approves in writing (TXT-035). Gate P and build_hold are unchanged.

- **D-323** Question (homeowner v9 N1, #1; trace S5, S22): emergency job cards reach businesses only by email, including at night, and a night emergency depends on a tradesperson noticing an ordinary-looking email. Choice: CARD-021: for a job chosen as an emergency, each card notice has the subject "Ask Hava: EMERGENCY <category> job in <town>. Quote as soon as you can." and places one automated call from the Ask Hava number to the owner's phone and each "Cards and quotes" phone with emergency-job calls on: "This is Ask Hava LLC. New emergency <trade> job in <town>, job <n>. The homeowner chose emergency. Check your email or askhava.com to quote. Press 1 to hear this again.", with the callback number, no homeowner details, no retry, held or sent with its notice, so at night only after-hours businesses are called (QH-004, QH-005); CARD-020 points to it; BIZ-007: "after-hours emergencies" can be ticked only while at least one such phone has calls on, and says "We call you about emergency jobs at any hour, so you can quote fast."; BIZ-037: each person switches emergency-job calls; CON-019: a separate unchecked box for emergency-job calls at any hour the business takes after-hours calls, "Not required to join.", with its own version and time; A11Y-024 and OPS-004 cover these calls. Gating: the same as the pick call (D-310), written E-SIGN consent from the business, with the TCPA attorney's confirmation already on Casey's list (D-322) widened to cover these calls (D-343); business automated calls have never had a lawyer switch (the CALL-019 switch is for homeowner safety calls placed on a keyword alone, D-275), so none is added. Reason: email is "slow; often unread" (messaging research sections 5 and 6) and the night card is the step that decides whether help comes tonight; a separate optional box also meets the "not a condition of purchase" part of prior express written consent if such calls were ever treated as telemarketing (47 CFR 64.1200(f)(9)); the 8 a.m. to 9 p.m. limit in 64.1200(c)(1) is for telephone solicitations to residential subscribers, and the business asks for night calls by ticking after-hours; business consent covers automated calls (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:181`); the identity-call opening is reused (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:182`); businesses tick after-hours (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:87`).
- **D-324** Question (homeowner v9 N1 by day, N7, N8; #4): the night count says "1 HVAC businesses ... take", the 60-minute "no quote yet" text runs only at night, the daytime emergency wording says "reach you tonight" at 3 p.m., and "Today" jobs aren't told quotes come by email. Choice: INT-033: a singular template for N of 1 and the count's businesses always have a CARD-021 phone; a daytime 60-minute text, "Ask Hava: No business has quoted yet. If it can't wait, it's fine to call a company you know. If anyone feels unwell or it isn't safe, call 911."; INT-006 daytime: "Emergency: businesses get a phone call about your request, and the business you pick has 60 minutes to confirm. Businesses that take after-hours calls can also come tonight. After-hours work usually costs more."; TXT-032 adds "Businesses get requests by email, so the first quote can take a few hours." on non-emergency "Today" jobs. Reason: honest expectations; the call-a-company-you-know valve already exists (D-241); emergency flow (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:86`).
- **D-325** Question (homeowner v9 N2, #2; trace S16, S17, S19): "gas leak", "gas line is hissing", "fuga de gas", "huele a gas", burst pipes and "my detector is going off" get no safety text, and there is no AI at launch (AI-008), so the keyword list is the only net. Choice: INT-002 adds the generic smell words (odor, odour, stink, fumes, huele, oler, apesta) and the phrases below; INT-004 defines a gas match (propane; gas with leak, leaking, hiss, whistl, odor, smell, stink or fumes; rotten egg; sulfur; "fuga de gas", "escape de gas", "huele a gas", "olor a gas", "sale gas", silb with gas, "huevo podrido", azufre) and widens the CO match ("CO" as a word with alarm, detector, beep or going off; any alarm or detector said to be sounding, in English or Spanish, with no smoke or fire word) and the symptom words (headache, nauseous, vomit, throwing up, mareado, mareada, "dolor de cabeza", vómit); INT-034 adds fire, flame, fuego, incendio, "en llamas" and shock words ("got shocked", "electric shock", electrocut, "me dio toque", "descarga eléctrica") to the electrical match, burst, broken pipe, "water main", "water everywhere", pouring, gushing, spraying, revent, "tubo roto", "tubería rota" and "agua por todos lados" to the water match, and a smoke-alarm line, "If a smoke alarm is sounding, get everyone outside and call 911 from outside. Stay out until responders say it's safe."; "fire" never in "fire pit", "fire table" or "fire sprinkler"; a chirp alone never counts as sounding; every new match holds the card until SAFE like the others (INT-005). Reason: PHMSA lists a hissing or roaring sound and odor as leak signs; the Santa Cruz County Spanish sheet uses "huevos podridos" and "un silbido"; CPSC's Spanish CO page says "Si la alarma suena ... Salga al aire libre inmediatamente"; CDC's Spanish symptoms are "dolor de cabeza, mareos, debilidad, malestar estomacal, vómitos, dolor de pecho y confusión"; USFA says "get outside and stay outside" when a smoke alarm sounds; Kidde says a single chirp each minute means a low battery; the blueprint's list is a floor (`sources/01_spec/Havasu_Home_Quotes_Technical_Blueprint_v9.3.md:290`); the safety text stays generic (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:196`).
- **D-326** Question (homeowner v9 N10, #10): after SAFE on a gas match, the flow goes to quotes with no "has the gas company checked it?" step, and the business isn't told. Choice: INT-035: one direct reply, "Ask Hava: Has your gas company checked it? 1) Yes 2) Not yet", never holding the request; "Not yet" gets "Ask Hava: Please call your gas company before anyone works on it. Keep the appliance off."; CARD-007 adds the fixed field "Gas company checked: yes / not yet / no answer". Reason: utility guidance puts the gas company first (PHMSA; the Santa Cruz County sheet); fixed card fields only (D-276); a direct reply is allowed at night (QH-004).
- **D-327** Question (homeowner v9 N6, #6; trace S20, S21): the 30-minute emergency reminder may not call, "you can choose another quote" is said when there is none, and an emergency pick made just before 8 p.m. of a business without after-hours calls can lapse with little real reach. Choice: BIZ-039: the reminder at 60 minutes, or 30 minutes on an emergency job, places the pick call; PICK-044: with no other choosable quote the homeowner reads "Ask Hava: If they don't confirm by <time>, we'll text you. More businesses may still quote."; a pick before 8 p.m. whose 60-minute window ends after 8 p.m., of a business without after-hours, warns "Ask Hava: <Business> doesn't take after-hours calls and may not see your pick after 8 p.m. To reach someone tonight, choose a quote marked Takes after-hours calls.", the notice and call going at once and the reminder held by quiet hours. Reason: true wording at the moment it matters; emergency flow (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:86`).
- **D-328** Question (business v9 4.1, E01 and E03: 21 and 20 of 29 personas, 22 quitting rows; P17 blocked by the verification email in Yahoo spam; gap 5.2): the spec has no spam-folder step, test email or sender guidance. Choice: BIZ-042: the verification screen says "No email? Check your Spam or Junk folder." with resend; a sample card email ("Ask Hava: Sample job card (not a real job)", fake data, the real sender) asks "Did it arrive in your inbox?" and every answer but Inbox shows that provider's steps (Gmail: Not spam, add to contacts, a filter with "Never send it to Spam", move to Primary; Outlook: Not junk and Safe senders; Yahoo: Not spam; iPhone Mail: VIP), with resend; team members see it at first sign-in; the owner signs in once on their own phone; nothing blocks activation; SEC-025: one fixed sender name, "Ask Hava", and one fixed address; CON-020: the welcome email repeats the sender, the steps, the calling number and "Ask Hava emails come only from @askhava.com, never ask for your card or a code, and every button goes to askhava.com.". Reason: Gmail Help's own fix is "Not spam", contacts and a "Never send it to Spam" filter; Microsoft's is Safe senders; a stable sender keeps those rules working; business sign-in by emailed link (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:315`); emails as real text (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:419`).
- **D-329** Question (business v9 4.1(5); P16 weekday-only inbox, P02 IT quarantine, X07): one inbox is a single point of failure. Choice: BIZ-043: each person may add one backup notice address, verified before it gets anything, receiving that person's card, open-cards, pick, reminder and money emails with the same person-bound links; changes follow SEC-024; the release goes to it only for the owner or a "Confirm and billing" member. Reason: a second inbox the person controls is the remedy once a provider stops sending to an address that complained; the same person and roles, so no new access (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:315`).
- **D-330** Question (business v9 4.1(3); P07, P19, P10 read in batches): cards expire unseen while the window runs. Choice: CARD-022: one open-cards email a day at the person's chosen time (8 a.m., noon or 5 p.m.; 5 p.m. by default), only when they have unquoted cards with at least 1 hour left, listing trade, town, end time and Quote link, switchable off, email only, inside quiet hours, never to a paused or booked business, never marketing; the window is unchanged. Reason: a reminder inside the window reaches batch readers without changing the homeowner's promised end time (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:83`, `sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:184`).
- **D-331** Question (business v9 4.1(4), 4.5): nothing tells a business it missed cards, and the app-with-push decision (BIZ-041) has no data. Choice: BIZ-025 adds "cards that ended without a quote"; BIZ-044: a monthly account email only when a business got at least 3 cards and at least half ended without a quote (settings), naming the counts and pointing to the BIZ-042 steps and a backup address, switchable off; OPS-008 shows the share of cards that ended without a quote, which feeds the BIZ-041 trigger in `docs/for-casey-review.md`. Reason: account notices under CON-019, never marketing; "ended without a quote" is measurable without open tracking (D-332).
- **D-332** Question (business v9 4.1(7), 4.7, X08; gaps 5.3, 5.4): should the 24-hour quote window count from first open or be longer, what counts as "sent", and what proof does a business see? Choice: keep 24 hours from send (PICK-001, D-042, D-308), with no open or click tracking; PICK-019: when every email of a pick notice is reported bounced or failed and its pick call failed or wasn't placed (an unanswered call counts as placed), a non-emergency pick gets 1 more business hour, once, and the homeowner gets the PICK-043 text; a delivery to spam counts as sent; BIZ-045: a notice log on each job page with each notice's send time or quiet-hours hold, the provider's status per address ("Delivered means your mail server accepted it; it may still be in Spam.") and each automated call's outcome. Reason: Apple Mail Privacy Protection downloads tracking pixels when it caches mail, so "opens" can read 100% (Postmark), and scanners open links, so "first open" would be neither honest nor private and would make the homeowner's "They have until <time>" unknowable; a longer window mostly helps businesses that never look; the grace covers only failures the business can't see or fix; delivery status is already tracked (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:140`).
- **D-333** Question (business v9 4.2, E05: 26 of 29; X07; gap 5.1): the Quote link's session rule is unstated, Confirm needs a full sign-in under a 2-hour clock, in-app browsers drop sessions, mail scanners can open single-use links first, and the Hold link acts on open. Choice: BIZ-038: the Quote link opens that job's form for that person without a session through a signed single-purpose link used up only on submit and working only until the card's window ends; Confirm needs a session or, from the person's own pick email, only a Verify code (SEC-004: a correct code also signs that device in); every action email carries the "Open in browser" line; SEC-026: opening any action link (a GET) never acts or uses it up, only the page's button (a POST with a CSRF token) acts, tokens are at least 128 random bits, stored hashed, never logged, and their pages send no referrer; A11Y-023 drops the Hold exception; SEC-027: a "This inbox is shared" switch makes every Confirm by that person need a fresh phone code. Reason: Quote moves no money, like Hold (D-309); Confirm keeps two factors, the person's own inbox and their phone; scanners in institutional mail open links and use up one-time tokens (Supabase discussion #41618), so acting only on a button keeps links working and stops a scanner from holding a pick; ASVS L2 (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:293`); business sign-in (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:315`).
- **D-334** Question (business v9 4.3, E04: 22 of 29; P01, P08, P11, P18, A01, A06; gap 5.6): the pick call comes from an unknown number, may ring the wrong person, and its voicemail doesn't say what kind of job. Choice: BIZ-046: every automated business call shows the Ask Hava number; the done screen and welcome email offer a contact card named "Ask Hava"; a test call at activation to each phone with pick or emergency-job calls on, with "We couldn't reach the phone ending <last 4>." and "Call me again" (at most 3 a day) when it isn't answered with press 1; BIZ-037: the setup screen asks "Who should get pick calls?" and "Who should get emergency-job calls?"; BIZ-039: the call says "You were picked for a <trade> job, job <n>."; CON-019 names account calls. Reason: people screen unknown numbers; a saved contact shows a name without paying for branded caller ID (a purchase left to Casey, D-343); the identity-call opening and callback number are reused (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:182`); consent to automated calls (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:181`).
- **D-335** Question (business v9 4.1(6); gap 5.7): after a spam complaint the provider stops sending to that address, and a solo business with no other address isn't told. Choice: BIZ-040: when an address turns unverified and the person has no other verified address, one automated account call, inside quiet hours and never to an opted-out phone: "This is Ask Hava LLC. Ask Hava emails to <business> are being returned or marked as spam, so you may miss job requests. Sign in at askhava.com/business to fix your email."; CON-019 names it. Reason: the only channel left is the phone the business consented to; no homeowner details (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:181`).
- **D-336** Question (business v9 4.8; X05, A01, X06): a refund denial gives no reason, and the public business name isn't screened like "About us". Choice: FEE-022: the decision email says which condition was or wasn't met, with the "Ask about a charge" form for questions; BIZ-022: the public business name on quote cards is checked against the same channel, phone and link rules. Reason: refunds are decided by fixed rules, so the reason can be stated; the name is the remaining way around PICK-007 (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:13`).
- **D-337** Question (homeowner v9 N3, #3; H16, A03, A04, A05, H18): "A person reads HELP messages within 4 days" is honest but reads as "nobody's there". Choice: CON-018, CON-027, CON-031 and WEB-008 say "Casey, the local owner, reads HELP messages twice a week, and within 24 hours if your job is urgent." (Spanish: "Casey, el dueño local, lee los mensajes de AYUDA dos veces por semana, y en 24 horas si su trabajo es urgente."); the due dates are unchanged (4 days, 24 hours when urgent) and CON-018 states them. Reason: the same promise in kept terms (OPS-033 keeps Casey's two weekly windows at most 4 days apart, D-279) with a named local person, who is already named on the site (WEB-021); "Reply CALL ME" was not adopted because it adds Casey's time (D-342).
- **D-338** Question (homeowner v9 N4, #5; H01, A03): people who can't open links read the link first and stop before the LIST line. Choice: TXT-016: the LIST line comes before the quote-page link, which ends the text as "Or see them at: <link>". Reason: the fallback is useless after the point where these readers stop; LIST already exists (D-290).
- **D-339** Question (homeowner v9 N5, #7; H17): a homeowner who chose email contact still reads "will call / call them back". Choice: TXT-027's email-contact variant: "Ask Hava: <Business> confirmed request <n>. They'll email you at the address you chose. Ask for the price and any changes in writing before work starts. Don't pay in full up front." Reason: true to the chosen channel, which deaf homeowners rely on; the safety lines stay (D-213).
- **D-340** Question (homeowner v9 #11; H14): a homeowner in a thin trade gets no early warning that quotes may not come. Choice: TXT-032: when the card went to 1 or 2 businesses (a setting), the first text adds "Only <N> <trade> businesses are on Ask Hava so far, so quotes may be slow or may not come." (singular for 1). Reason: a count, not a ranking (D-188); honest expectations reduce silent drop-off.
- **D-341** Question: which tasks build this? Choice: two new Stage 4 build tasks: T149 (business email reliability: the deliverability step, backup address, open-cards digest, missed-card email and notice log) after T147 and T148, and T150 (emergency-job calls, test and account calls, the contact card, and the action-link hardening of SEC-026 and SEC-027) after T148 and T149, both before the J2 journey (T099) and the Stage 4 clean-up (T100); changed requirements stay with the tasks that own them (T039 takes INT-035), and the owning tasks' prompts list these decisions. Reason: small PRs (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:485`); a task may share a file only with a task it follows.
- **D-342** Question: which proposals are not adopted? Choice: not adopted: counting the quote window from first open, extending it, and any email open or click tracking (D-332); "Reply CALL ME" call-backs for homeowners (Casey's time); a recurring weekly "Away" schedule (interview C5 first); owner approval of staff Confirms (interview C25, D-269); water keywords without the SAFE hold (shock risk); "chirp" as an alarm word (a low battery); a deadline time-of-day question (one persona; homeowner test 6 first); a trade-open reminder 7 days later (it would break "We'll tell you once", BIZ-031); business texting, WhatsApp or the app now (TXT-035, D-321; a data trigger goes to Casey instead); and, unchanged, tap-to-ask, three site visits, coverage refusal before consent, a PROBLEM consequence, "text me first" and a hashed number (D-301). Reason: plan section 3.
- **D-343** Question: what needs Casey? Choice: listed in `docs/for-casey-review.md`: widen the TCPA attorney question (pick, emergency-job, test and account calls; the separate optional box; Casey phoning a homeowner live during a texting suspension); decide whether to buy branded caller ID or CNAM for the Ask Hava number; run the inbox placement test with inboxes he owns before the first sign-up; walk founding businesses through the sample-card step in launch week; decide the BIZ-041 trigger (more than 25% of cards ending without a quote in the first 60 days, or more than 3 of the first 10 businesses citing missed emails); fix the pitch file outside the repo; add the new safety wording to the fire and gas desk check; and run interviews C27 to C31 and the "twice a week" homeowner test. Reason: BUILD_BRIEF §16 (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:542`); no purchases or outreach without Casey (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:61`).

**Spec changes from fix round 9:** `intake.md` INT-002, INT-004, INT-006, INT-033, INT-034 changed, INT-035 added; `job-cards.md` CARD-007, CARD-020 changed, CARD-021, CARD-022 added; `businesses.md` BIZ-007, BIZ-022, BIZ-025, BIZ-037 to BIZ-040 changed, BIZ-042 to BIZ-046 added; `consent.md` CON-018, CON-019, CON-020, CON-027, CON-031 changed; `website.md` WEB-008 changed; `quiet-hours.md` QH-004 changed; `quotes-pick.md` PICK-019, PICK-044 changed; `messaging.md` TXT-016, TXT-027, TXT-032 changed; `security.md` SEC-004, SEC-025 changed, SEC-026, SEC-027 added; `accessibility.md` A11Y-023, A11Y-024 changed; `ops-recovery.md` OPS-004, OPS-008 changed; `fees.md` FEE-022 changed.

## Fix round 10, October 28, 2026

Casey, Oct 28, 2026, 07:20: "No calls to businesses. We need to set up a help function that sends me a notification so I can fix it. No name attached." Earlier the same day: "Ok fix everything we need to." Inputs, all outside this repository: the round-9 plan (`/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v9/Fix_Round_9_Plan.md`), the messaging research (`/home/claude/hhq/outputs/messaging_options/Messaging_Risk_and_Options.md`), and two round-2 research reports dated October 28, 2026 (ChatGPT: local safety contacts, email deliverability, scanner-safe links, TCPA; Grok: tradespeople and email, Gmail tabs, local naming risks). The plan is `/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v10/Fix_Round_10_Plan.md`. Research is research, not legal advice; nothing was bought, sent or signed; Gate P and build_hold are unchanged.

- **D-344** Question (Casey 07:20, "No calls to businesses"): which calls stop? Choice: every automated call to a business phone is removed: the pick call and its press 1 and press 2 (BIZ-039 now states the rule), the emergency-job call (CARD-021), the activation test call and the "your emails stopped" account call (BIZ-046, BIZ-040); CON-019 asks no automated-call consent and its separate emergency-call box is gone, its wording saying "Ask Hava never places recorded or automated calls to you."; BIZ-017, BIZ-018, BIZ-036, BIZ-037, BIZ-045, CON-020, QH-004, QH-005, OPS-004, OPS-008, A11Y-024, TXT-034, WEB-024 and TERMS-021 drop the calls; the contact card stays only as "Ask Hava homeowner calls", listing the masked pool numbers so homeowner calls through Ask Hava are recognized (BIZ-046). Kept, because they are not automated calls to businesses: masked calls between a homeowner and a picked business (person to person, CALL-002); a Twilio Verify code call, now placed only when the person taps "Call me with the code" on screen (BIZ-002, BIZ-026, BIZ-046), which BUILD_BRIEF requires for landlines (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:417`, `BUILD_BRIEF.md:449`); the homeowner safety voice call, still off until a TCPA attorney's written approval is recorded (CALL-019), since Casey's instruction is about businesses; and the critical-alert call to Casey's own phone (OPS-035). Reason: Casey's instruction; it also removes the TCPA exposure the round-2 research flagged for prerecorded calls to business cell phones (47 CFR 64.1200(a)(1)(iii), (b); the "emergency" label doesn't create an exemption) and the attorney question for those calls.
- **D-345** Question: without a pick call, how does Confirm work by email alone? Choice: PICK-019: the non-emergency Confirm window is 4 business hours (was 2; a setting), counted from the pick email's send; PICK-037: a reminder 1 business hour after sending and a last reminder 1 business hour before the end ("Ask Hava: Last reminder: confirm job <n> by <time>, or the homeowner may choose another quote."), to the main and backup addresses (BIZ-043); CARD-022: the daily open-cards email also lists picks waiting for Confirm; HOLD (+2 business hours, link only, PICK-043) and Away (BIZ-034) unchanged; the bounce grace (+1 business hour once) now needs only every email to have bounced or failed; PICK-045: the homeowner is told at the pick "<Business> gets your pick by email and has until <time> to confirm. Businesses check email at different times. You can still choose another quote before then."; PICK-046: on a lapse, one text lists the other choosable quotes inline with "reply the number and YES" (a pick with no code, PICK-015), or says "We'll text you when another quote comes in" with the call-a-company line; JOB-013 follows. Reason: the 2-hour window was set with the call as the fast channel; with email only, the research found no study of how fast tradespeople see email, and that owner-operators read it at lunch or night (Grok round 2, part 1); Gmail alerts only for Primary; 4 business hours covers a lunch or end-of-day check while the homeowner keeps the right to switch at any time (PICK-032) and is told the real end time; measurement (D-347) can tune the setting.
- **D-346** Question: how do emergencies work without calls, day and night? Choice: CARD-021: the EMERGENCY subject plus an "EMERGENCY:" first line; by day it goes to every card recipient; at night only to people with the new per-person switch "Emergency emails at any hour" (BIZ-037) at businesses that ticked after-hours, others at 8 a.m.; BIZ-007: the after-hours tick needs at least one person who can confirm with that switch on and a Yes to "Did your phone alert you?" after a test emergency email (BIZ-042); emergency picks get the subject "Ask Hava: EMERGENCY pick: job <n>. Confirm by <time>." and go at night to those people (PICK-037, BIZ-037); the 60-minute emergency window, 30-minute reminder email and 60-minute "no quote yet" texts stay (PICK-044, INT-033); INT-006's daytime wording says businesses get the request "by email marked EMERGENCY"; PICK-044's homeowner texts say the pick goes by email; the INT-033 count counts only businesses with such a person; a lapsed emergency pick gets the PICK-046 re-pick text at once at any hour (QH-004). Reason: a business that offers after-hours work must prove its phone alerts on an Ask Hava email before it is counted, so the "takes after-hours calls" count stays honest; the homeowner is told the channel truthfully and keeps 911 and "call a company you know" lines; the re-pick path needs one reply, not a page visit.
- **D-347** Question: do the 24-hour quote window and the night flow still hold, and how is email speed known? Choice: PICK-001 unchanged (24 hours from send; D-332's reasons stand); the night emergency flow walked end to end (plan section 5) with no step depending on a call; OPS-037: from day one the app records send, provider-delivered, first opening of the person's own Quote or Confirm page (the app's own page request, marked "may be a mail scanner"; no pixel, no click redirect), quote and Confirm times, shown as weekly medians on the health page and per business in the notice log (BIZ-045), and the business data page says so (TERMS-021). Reason: Grok round 2 recommends measuring sent-to-open-to-quote from day one; D-332 rejected open tracking because pixels are faked by Apple Mail and add tracking, while a first visit to Ask Hava's own action page is a server record the business itself caused, disclosed and labeled as possibly a scanner.
- **D-348** Question (Casey 07:20, "a help function that sends me a notification so I can fix it"): how do HELP and business help reach Casey without using up his time? Choice: OPS-036: each help request (a homeowner message after HELP that isn't auto-answered, a help email or help-page form message, a business "Get help" or "Ask about a charge" message or email reply) sends one HELP notification at once, at any hour, as an email to his alert address, which the owner guide sets to notify on his phone (OPS-035's existing channel), never breaking Do Not Disturb and never a text or call; it carries the job or business number, status, trade, town, urgency, the message with contact details removed, the last automated message and one link to an owner-portal page (sign-in needed) with the timeline, the fixes he can make there and a reply box (his own words, screened, sent as "Ask Hava: Reply from the Ask Hava owner: ..."); follow-ups within an hour join the open request, more than 10 notifications a day wait for the 9 a.m./3 p.m. push (OPS-032), overdue requests go in that push; CON-034: the message after HELP is first matched by plain words against four common questions (where are my quotes, how to close, does it cost anything, is this a person) and auto-answered, opening a request only if the person writes again; CON-018, BIZ-032, BIZ-047, TERMS-022, WEB-008, WEB-021: the honest promise becomes "The Ask Hava owner gets HELP messages right away and replies within 2 days, or within 24 hours if your job is urgent." (2 days a setting; was "twice a week", 4 days), and while his away setting is on the reply says when he's back and to call 911 if anyone is in danger (OPS-026). Reason: Casey asked for a notification; the email-to-phone channel already exists and respects his Do Not Disturb (OPS-029); auto-answers and the hourly and daily limits keep his time small; a notification he acts on lets the promise shrink from 4 to 2 days honestly.
- **D-349** Question (Casey 07:20, "No name attached"): where does his name appear? Choice: no template, page, email or document names him: "Casey, the local owner" in CON-018, CON-027, CON-031 and WEB-008 becomes "the Ask Hava owner"; BIZ-030 and BIZ-032's "Casey reviews/reads" lines, TERMS-021 and TERMS-023's "Casey does not use Ask Hava data" and WEB-021/WEB-022's "Who runs Ask Hava" block (name and photo removed: "Ask Hava is run by its local owner, who lives in Havasu.") follow; WORD-021 makes it a rule with a scan against a personal-name list. Internal requirements still say Casey (they are not user-facing). Reason: Casey's instruction; the LLC address and help email keep the page accountable without a personal name.
- **D-350** Question (ChatGPT round 2, part 1): which local contacts go in safety texts, and how are they kept current? Choice: INT-036: one version-controlled file, `app/messaging/safety_contacts.toml`, with each entry's source URL, last-verified date and review-by date: UniSource's published 24-hour gas and electric emergency number ("verify before launch"), Lake Havasu City's published water and sewer number and its after-hours dispatch number (only as "if you're on city water"); the digits are in the plan, not in this repository, because the PII scan rejects real numbers, and T152 adds an exact-match allowance for this file's numbers only, with a test that every other real number still fails, 211 Arizona (live operators 9 a.m.-7 p.m., English and Spanish, funding reported through early 2027 by KJZZ, July 14, 2026; review by January 15, 2027); unverified, stale or expired entries drop out of every text and a Gate C checklist check fails while any is unverified; INT-004: the gas line follows UniSource and CPSC wording ("leave now; don't use switches, flames or phones near it; from outside call 911 and your gas company (UniSource natural gas: <number>); don't try to turn the meter or valves"), the CO line follows CPSC ("get everyone outside into fresh air right away and call 911; don't go back inside until responders say it's safe"); INT-031: the heat text adds CDC's "a fan alone isn't enough when it's over 90°F inside", cool showers or wet cloths, and restores 211 with its hours, and never names a cooling center or says one is open; INT-034: electrical adds downed or sparking lines reported to UniSource; water uses "if you know where your main water shutoff is and can reach it safely, turn it off" and the city numbers only "if you're on city water". Reason: the report's sources (UniSource contact, outage and gas-safety pages; lhcaz.gov water and dispatch pages; 211arizona.org; CDC, CPSC); D-274 removed 211 because its hours weren't confirmed, and they now are; a Lake Havasu address doesn't guarantee city water; a cooling center's open status can't be verified automatically.
- **D-351** Question (ChatGPT round 2, part 2): how are emails sent and links kept scanner-safe? Choice: SEC-025: sender "Ask Hava" <jobs@notify.askhava.com> on the dedicated sending subdomain notify.askhava.com, Reply-To the monitored help address (which opens help requests, OPS-036), never no-reply; SPF and DKIM aligned; DMARC p=none with reports read, then p=quarantine and p=reject before the first real business email (replacing an immediate p=reject); provider open and click tracking off; plain-text part, under 100 KB; no BIMI, VMC or dedicated IP; Resend's free tier at launch (3,000 a month, 100 a day) with a portal and digest alert at 80% of either (OPS-004), upgrading being Casey's purchase; SEC-026 now covers every emailed link, homeowner ones included: a GET only shows the page, changes no state and doesn't use up or shorten the token; each action is a POST from a button, idempotent (acts once, same result on repeat). Reason: report part 2 (Gmail, Yahoo and Microsoft sender rules; Resend's DMARC, tracking and pricing pages; Microsoft Safe Links and Supabase on link prefetching; DigiCert VMC cost with no ROI at pilot scale); CON-020's "only from @askhava.com" becomes "addresses ending in askhava.com" to cover the subdomain.
- **D-352** Question (Grok round 2): which of its points are adopted? Choice: BIZ-042's last onboarding step "Reply OK to this email, star it and add us to your contacts" with a picture guide per mail app (Gmail: Primary tab, since the app alerts only for Primary; iPhone Mail VIP; Outlook Safe senders and Focused; Yahoo contacts), the OK reply recorded and never a command; timing measured from day one (OPS-037, D-347); WEB-008 and WEB-025: "Ask Hava isn't connected to any business with "Hava" in its name."; WORD-021: always "Ask Hava", never "Hava" alone (WEB-008's "Is Hava a person?" becomes "Is Ask Hava a person?"); WORD-020: Spanish copy never says "leads", "clientes potenciales" or "prospectos"; the trademark-attorney item adds Hav-A-Handyman LLC and Hava-Guy Handyman. Reason: Gmail Help (notifications only for Primary; starring, contacts and replies train the tabs); local "Hava" trade names create confusion risk both ways.
- **D-353** Question: which tasks build this? Choice: T148 is retitled to Confirm by email (window, reminders, PICK-045, PICK-046, and the email sender rules TD-SEC-09) and owns `app/picks/confirm_reminders.py` and its test instead of the pick-call files; T150 is retitled to emergency emails, the no-business-call guard, on-tap Verify voice, the homeowner-calls contact card and scanner-safe idempotent links, owning `app/picks/emergency_emails.py` and `tests/integration/test_emergency_card_email.py` and `tests/integration/test_no_business_calls.py` instead of the call files; T149 adds OPS-037 and `app/messaging/email_timing.py`; new T151 (Stage 5, after T105, T102 and T082) builds help requests, auto-answers, the owner help page and the business "Get help" form (CON-034, OPS-036, BIZ-047); new T152 (Stage 2, before T039) builds the safety contacts file, its loader and launch check (INT-036); T104 adds WORD-021 and WEB-025 to the wording scan; changed requirements stay with their owning tasks, whose prompts list these decisions. Reason: small PRs (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:485`); a task may share a file only with a task it follows; T039 and T102 are at their test and requirement limits.
- **D-354** Question: what is not adopted? Choice: not adopted: a text nudge to businesses (TXT-035 unchanged until Twilio answers in writing); branded caller ID or CNAM (no business calls left to brand); keeping any business call behind a lawyer switch (Casey said none); counting the quote window from first open or lengthening it (D-332); an AI reading of HELP messages (plain word matching only); naming a cooling center; assuming city water; BIMI, a VMC or a dedicated IP (cost, no ROI); pixel or click tracking; a homeowner "call and leave a message" intake and keeping askhava.com/seniors (both listed as Casey's call, not adopted). Reason: Casey's 07:20 instruction, cost, and the research's own recommendations.
- **D-355** Question: what needs Casey? Choice: listed in `docs/for-casey-review.md`: the TCPA attorney question narrows to the homeowner safety voice call and Casey phoning a homeowner during a texting suspension (business calls are gone); the branded caller ID purchase item is withdrawn; verify the UniSource number and the city water numbers before launch (and 211 by January 15, 2027); ask the city about private-shutoff wording and the public/private water boundary; check the HELP promise of 2 days is one he can keep (or set it back); set his phone to notify on the alert address; the Resend upgrade if the free tier's 100 a day is reached (a purchase); the pitch file's "we call you when a homeowner picks you" line; the trademark attorney list adds Hav-A-Handyman LLC and Hava-Guy Handyman; decide on a "call and leave a message" homeowner intake and on keeping askhava.com/seniors. Reason: BUILD_BRIEF §16 (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:542`); no purchases or outreach without Casey (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:61`).

## Fix round 11, October 28, 2026

Casey, Oct 28, 2026, 08:01: "Ask Hava is not a contractor. All we are doing is taking the quotes from contractors and relaying that information to the customers. We are not subcontracting them... whatever we need to change in our verbiage to make that blatantly obvious, we need to do... All we're doing is just working as an assistant to take the information from the customers, relay that information to contractors and other businesses, and then format all of the quotes and give that back to the customer with all the necessary information and let them make the decision." Earlier the same day: "Ok fix everything we need to." Inputs, all outside this repository: the v10 homeowner findings (`/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v10/homeowner/Homeowner_Angle_Findings_v10.md`), the ChatGPT round 3 research of October 28, 2026 (Part 2 Stripe, Part 3 LLC records, Part 4 water providers) and Casey's instructions on owner identity and professional services. The plan is `/home/claude/hhq/outputs/sim_v10/Fix_Round_11_Plan.md`. Research is research, not legal advice; nothing was bought, sent or signed; Gate P and build_hold are unchanged.

- **D-356** Question (Casey 08:01, "make that blatantly obvious"): what does every surface say, and where? Choice: one standard statement in three lengths, in English and Spanish (Spanish marked for native-speaker review): the long one, "Ask Hava is not a contractor and does not perform, bid on or supervise work. We pass along your request and show you quotes from independent local businesses, as they wrote them. You choose, and you hire the business directly.", on every site page (footer) and the help page, the homeowner terms, the privacy policy and the quote page (WORD-002, WEB-015, TERMS-004); the short one, "Ask Hava LLC is a local quote service in Lake Havasu City, not a contractor. We pass your request to local businesses, and you hire the one you choose directly.", in the consent text, the first text on every path (CON-031); the business one, "Ask Hava is not a contractor. It passes along this request and the homeowner's choice. If the homeowner picks you, they hire you directly; Ask Hava doesn't direct, schedule, supervise or pay for the work.", on every job card, the business page, the sign-up and the business terms (CARD-020, BIZ-030, TERMS-026); and the pick review step adds "You hire the business directly. Ask Hava isn't part of your agreement with it." (PICK-017). Reason: Casey's instruction; the long version keeps the source sentence word for word (`sources/01_spec/Changes_Since_v9.3_AUTHORITATIVE.md:237`, `BUILD_BRIEF.md:453`), so WORD-002 and WEB-015 still hold; texts stay short by using only the short version, inside a text that is already sent.
- **D-357** Question: what must the business terms say about independence and the fee? Choice: new TERMS-026: businesses are independent; Ask Hava never directs, controls, schedules, supervises, inspects, warrants or pays for their work, never sets or changes their prices and never negotiates for either side; there is no subcontract, employment, agency, partnership or joint venture; the homeowner and the business make their own agreement and Ask Hava is not part of it; the introduction fee pays only for the introduction service, is fixed by job size ($25, $75 or $199), is never a percentage or share of the job's price, and doesn't depend on whether the work is done, finished or paid for, the only refunds being those the terms list (FEE-022 to FEE-025); FEE-002 and FEE-006 point to it; BIZ-030's summary adds "It's a fixed introduction fee, never a share of the job, and it doesn't depend on the work being done or paid for." Reason: Casey's instruction ("We are not subcontracting them"); the existing fee rules already match (FEE-001, FEE-002, FEE-006), so this states them where a business reads them.
- **D-358** Question: does product behaviour match the words? Choice: the audit found no feature that edits a quote's price, negotiates or sets prices; one display gap is fixed: TXT-016 and TXT-031 showed recurring amounts "per month", converting a business's weekly or yearly price into Ask Hava's own figure, and now show each amount and period exactly as the business stated it, the monthly conversion (REC-004, REC-010) being used only for the fee tier; WORD-011 adds that Ask Hava never edits, rounds, converts or drops a quote's amount, never suggests a price to a business, never passes a counter-offer, and only the business changes its quote (PICK-006's read-back being the business confirming its own words); the FEE-033 flag stays owner-only and never messages a business about its prices. Reason: Casey's instruction ("format all of the quotes and give that back"): formatting may not change what the business said.
- **D-359** Question: which existing words imply Ask Hava hires, books, routes workers or that businesses work through it? Choice: the sweep of every requirement and both catalog files changes: PICK-037's subject "You were picked for job <n>" becomes "A homeowner chose your quote for job <n>"; BIZ-028 and BIZ-038's "Booked until", "Booked for <n> days" and "booking" become "Busy until", "Busy for <n> days" and "busy setting", and CARD-022's "booked business" "business set to busy"; BIZ-046's "homeowners who picked you, connected through Ask Hava" becomes "homeowners who chose your quote, calling on an Ask Hava masked number" and BIZ-039's "through Ask Hava" "on an Ask Hava masked number"; CON-034's fee answer adds "a fixed introduction fee. You pay the business directly for the work."; the night label "takes after-hours calls" (it is email) becomes "gets emergency requests at night" in INT-006, INT-033 and PICK-044; the spec area "Job cards and matching" and test-design section "Matching and licensing" are renamed "Job cards and who receives them" and "Card eligibility and licensing"; the catalog had no hit (`routed_to` names which licensed businesses may receive a refused job type and stays); and the stale "plus a phone call when they're picked" in `docs/for-casey-review.md` is corrected. Reason: Casey's instruction; each phrase either described Ask Hava as placing workers ("booked", "connected through") or was untrue ("calls").
- **D-360** Question (Casey 08:01): how does the build keep the wording from coming back? Choice: new WORD-022 with one banned-phrase list, `NOT_A_CONTRACTOR` in `scripts/trace_spec.py`: Ask Hava hiring, dispatching, assigning, sending a worker, scheduling, booking, subcontracting, managing, matching and assigning, warranting, guaranteeing, pricing, bidding on or recommending work, and businesses working for or through Ask Hava ("our pros", "our network", "your contractor", "we'll send a plumber", "book", "hire through us", "through Ask Hava", "service provider", "we found you", "assigned", "subcontract", "guaranteed", "our price" and the Spanish "nuestros profesionales", "nuestra red", "le enviaremos un plomero", "subcontrat..."); `scripts/trace_spec.py` fails on any hit in a requirement's text except the five rules that quote the phrases to ban them (WORD-011, WORD-020, WORD-022, TERMS-012, TERMS-026); `scripts/check_catalog.py` adds the list to the catalog's banned wording; T104's app scan reads the same list for every template, page, email and drafted document in both languages; tests in `tests/test_trace_spec.py` and `tests/test_check_catalog.py` plant each kind of phrase and expect a failure. Reason: an instruction is kept only if the build enforces it; one list in one place keeps the spec check, the catalog check and the app scan the same.
- **D-361** Question (homeowner v10 fix #4): which safety lines are wrong? Choice: INT-004's "If you smell gas or hear a leak, leave now." becomes "If you smell gas or hear gas hissing, leave now.", and on a water-only match the water lines come first; a smoke or CO detector said to chirp, or to beep with a battery, low-battery or once-a-minute word, is a low-battery chirp with no 911 line and no SAFE hold, answered "Ask Hava: A smoke or CO detector that chirps about once a minute usually needs a new battery or a new unit. If it's sounding a loud alarm over and over, or anyone feels unwell, get everyone outside and call 911 from outside."; a smoke detector "beeping" with no sounding, smoke, fire or symptom word gets that text plus "Is it 1) a chirp every minute or so, or 2) a loud alarm that keeps sounding?", 2 starting the full safety text and anything else continuing as a regular job; a CO detector "beeping" with no chirp or battery word keeps the CO lines (INT-002, INT-004, INT-034); a gas match with propane, propano, "LP gas" or a tank word names "your propane supplier (the number is usually on the tank or your bill)" in place of UniSource natural gas, and INT-035 asks "Has your propane supplier or gas company checked it?"; and the heat text's 211 sentence is sent only while 211's live line is open (9 a.m. to 7 p.m. Arizona time, from the INT-036 entry) (INT-031). Reason: the findings' trace (S29 to S33) and personas H02, H03, H05 and H08: a water text told people hearing water to leave; a battery chirp got "call 911"; UniSource sells natural gas, not propane; 211 closed at night isn't help.
- **D-362** Question (homeowner v10 fix #1): the night EMERGENCY email rests on a one-time daytime phone-alert test; how is it kept reliable? Choice: BIZ-042: the test emergency email screen says "Turn on Do Not Disturb or Sleep the way your phone is at night, then send the test.", asks "Did your phone alert you with Do Not Disturb or Sleep on?" and shows per-phone steps (iPhone: Settings, Focus, Do Not Disturb and Sleep, allow your mail app; Android: Do Not Disturb exceptions, allow your mail app); a Yes lasts 90 days (a setting), after which the person gets a re-test email and stops counting until a new Yes; BIZ-007: when 2 night EMERGENCY cards in a row (a setting) get no visit to their own link pages (OPS-037) within 60 minutes from anyone with "Emergency emails at any hour" on, the after-hours tick is suspended, the owner is emailed with the re-test link, and the business leaves the INT-033 count until a new Yes; new test TD-BIZ-24. Reason: the spec itself says email notifications don't break Do Not Disturb (OPS-029, OPS-036); a test under the phone's night settings is the only honest proof without calls (D-344), and a business that misses night cards should not be counted for homeowners at night.
- **D-363** Question (homeowner v10 fix #2): late-evening picks wait overnight under the 4-business-hour window; what rule? Choice: "tell before, offer the faster path": PICK-017's review step shows the Confirm end time before the pick, "If you choose now, <Business> has until <time> to confirm by email.", adds "Businesses may not see email until morning." when that window runs past 8 p.m., and on a job answered "Today, it's urgent" but chosen regular, while the INT-033 night count is above 0, offers a one-tap "Switch to emergency (60-minute confirm)"; the window itself is unchanged (PICK-019); new test TD-PICK-28. Reason: H23 picked at 8:10 p.m. in heat without being told; telling before the pick and offering the faster path fixes the harm without changing the business-side window, which needs the business view (D-370).
- **D-364** Question (ChatGPT round 3, Part 4): which water contacts go in the burst-pipe text? Choice: INT-034's water line becomes "Stay out of standing water near outlets, cords or appliances. Don't stand in water to reach the breaker panel. If you know where your home's own main water shutoff is and can reach it safely, turn it off. Don't use the water company's meter as a shutoff. If anyone is in danger, call 911. For a leak at the street or meter, call your water provider; it's on your water bill: Lake Havasu City <number> (after hours <number>), EPCOR <number> (24 hours), Havasu Heights <number> (after hours). On a well or septic, call a plumber.", each provider phrase from the INT-036 file and left out when its entry can't be used, never saying which provider serves the home; INT-036 adds EPCOR's 24-hour emergency number (epcor.com Arizona contacts) and Havasu Heights Domestic Water Improvement District's after-hours number (hhdwid.com), each "verify before launch"; as in D-350, the digits are in the plan, not in this repository, because the PII scan rejects real numbers, and T152's exact-match allowance covers them; new test TD-INT-18. Reason: the report found three water providers in the area and that an address alone doesn't identify one; Havasu Heights warns against using its meter as a shutoff; septic homes have no sewer utility to call.
- **D-365** Question (ChatGPT round 3, Part 2): how does a business pay? Choice: confirm and pay are one on-session step on the pick page: the PICK-021 review step shows the job number, fee, tier and basis, the refund line and the terms version, and one "Confirm and pay $<fee>" button; Stripe Elements takes a new card or a saved one and the PaymentIntent is confirmed with the business present (3-D Secure in the page) (FEE-036, PICK-021); saving a card is an optional, unticked "Save this card for next time" with its own authorization, and no off-session charge exists in V1 (FEE-014, FEE-015); there are no background retries: each press is one attempt with its own idempotency key, a repeated press or callback reuses it, a decline or bank check shows on the page at once and the business can try another card until the card-fix time ends (FEE-016, FEE-020, JOB-014); details are released only after the server sees the payment succeed (a verified webhook or the PaymentIntent fetched with status succeeded), exactly once (PICK-022); a texted CONFIRM never charges and gets the Confirm link (PICK-033); Stripe's receipt goes with the Ask Hava receipt (FEE-031); and new FEE-042 keeps an evidence record per charge. The Stripe support question is drafted in `docs/for-casey-review.md`. `BUILD_BRIEF.md:110` lists "SetupIntent with off-session use" for payments; V1 still uses the stripe SDK, Stripe-hosted card fields (card data never reaches Ask Hava's servers) and webhooks, with the SetupIntent optional and no off-session charge, the owner's instruction deciding what is built; both lines and this choice are logged here (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:539`). Reason: the report recommends on-session confirmation in V1 (Stripe's save-and-reuse guidance asks for informed consent to off-session charges, which can also fail or need authentication); one deliberate action keeps the fee tied to the business's own Confirm.
- **D-366** Question (Casey's instructions on identity and calls): what does user-facing copy say about who runs Ask Hava, and which calls remain? Choice: no personal name anywhere user-facing (D-349 unchanged); where trust needs it, "Ask Hava LLC, a Lake Havasu City business" (WEB-021, WEB-022, CON-031); HELP keeps "replies within 2 days"; no call of any kind is placed to a business: the Verify voice code for businesses is withdrawn (BIZ-002, BIZ-026, BIZ-046, SEC-004, TXT-034, TXT-038), a landline record contact gets the identity code by email to the record address or by letter, and sign-in and Confirm codes go by text, or by email to the person's own sign-in address when they have no phone that gets texts; a homeowner gets a Verify voice code only after tapping "Call me with the code instead" on the screen (PICK-013, SEC-008, CALL-014, CALL-015); masked calls between a homeowner and the business it chose stay, being people calling each other and the refund's call attempts (CALL-002, FEE-022), and are flagged for Casey; new test TD-BIZ-25. `BUILD_BRIEF.md:417` ("The voice code call speaks slowly ...") and `BUILD_BRIEF.md:449` ("The identity voice call says "This is Ask Hava LLC" ...") assume business voice codes; the owner's instruction decides what is built, so they, and their restatement in `docs/constitution.md`, now apply only to the homeowner voice code. Reason: Casey's instruction; the ChatGPT round 3 report (Part 3) notes that Arizona LLC filings list members or managers publicly, so the copy names the LLC, not a person, and that limit is listed for Casey.
- **D-367** Question (Casey: no lawyers, CPA or broker for now): how are open legal and tax items handled? Choice: in `docs/for-casey-review.md`, every "hire an attorney", "ask a CPA" or "trademark attorney" item becomes "AI legal-research memo (in progress, `/home/claude/hhq/outputs/legal/`) + Casey's decision", with a stance section at the top; insurance stays a recommended purchase (it is cover, not legal advice); government filings and registrations (the LLC, the city license, a TPT license if needed, a trademark filing, the Twilio and Stripe accounts) stay Casey's own actions; the safety voice call (CALL-019) stays off, because its switch still needs a recorded attorney approval and changing that gate would be a spec change Casey has to ask for. Reason: Casey's instruction; Casey accepts the risk himself; no spec gate is weakened.
- **D-368** Question: which tasks build this? Choice: T104 takes WORD-002 and WORD-022 with new tests TD-WORD-05 (the statement everywhere) and TD-WORD-06 (the banned-phrase scan); T111 takes TERMS-026 with TD-TERMS-03; T047 takes WORD-011 with TD-TXT-21 (amounts as stated); T039 takes INT-037 and its TD-INT-01 adds the D-361 cases; T152 takes INT-034, INT-031, INT-035 and INT-004 with TD-INT-18 (water providers, propane contact, 211 hours); T150 takes BIZ-007, BIZ-042, BIZ-002, BIZ-026, SEC-004, SEC-008, PICK-013, TXT-038, CALL-014, CALL-015 and INT-033 with TD-BIZ-24 (night alert under Do Not Disturb) and TD-BIZ-25 (no business voice codes, homeowner voice code on tap); T055 takes PICK-045 and PICK-019 with TD-PICK-28 (late-evening picks); T062 takes FEE-014, FEE-015, FEE-036, PICK-021, PICK-022, PICK-033 and JOB-014 with TD-FEE-25 (on-session confirm and pay); T066 takes FEE-042 and FEE-031 with TD-FEE-26 (evidence records); T104 also takes CON-031, CARD-020, PICK-017, WEB-015, TERMS-004 and BIZ-030 for TD-WORD-05; T111 also takes FEE-002, FEE-006 and BIZ-030; T047 also takes PICK-002 and PICK-003; TD-FEE-10, TD-FEE-11, TD-BIZ-18, TD-BIZ-22 and TD-CALL-06 are updated; changed requirements stay with their owning tasks, whose prompts list these decisions. Reason: small PRs (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:485`); each new test goes to the task that owns the behaviour and has room under the 6-test limit.
- **D-369** Question: what is not adopted?
